% Naloga 1 
% Prikaz komponent HSV in Lab

clear all; close all; clc;

% 1) prebere sliki
img1 = imread('yosemite_meadows.jpg');
img2 = imread('coloured_pencils.jpg');


%   HSV ZA YOSEMITE

hsv1 = rgb2hsv(img1);

H1 = hsv1(:,:,1);
S1 = hsv1(:,:,2);
V1 = hsv1(:,:,3);

figure('Name','Yosemite - HSV');
subplot(1,3,1); imagesc(H1); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('H');
subplot(1,3,2); imagesc(S1); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('S');
subplot(1,3,3); imagesc(V1); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('V');


%  Lab ZA YOSEMITE

lab1 = rgb2lab(img1);

L1 = lab1(:,:,1);
a1 = lab1(:,:,2);
b1 = lab1(:,:,3);

figure('Name','Yosemite - Lab');
subplot(1,3,1); imagesc(L1); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('L');
subplot(1,3,2); imagesc(a1); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('a');
subplot(1,3,3); imagesc(b1); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('b');



% HSV ZA PENCILS

hsv2 = rgb2hsv(img2);

H2 = hsv2(:,:,1);
S2 = hsv2(:,:,2);
V2 = hsv2(:,:,3);

figure('Name','Pencils - HSV');
subplot(1,3,1); imagesc(H2); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('H');
subplot(1,3,2); imagesc(S2); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('S');
subplot(1,3,3); imagesc(V2); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('V');



%  Lab ZA PENCILS

lab2 = rgb2lab(img2);

L2 = lab2(:,:,1);
a2 = lab2(:,:,2);
b2 = lab2(:,:,3);

figure('Name','Pencils - Lab');
subplot(1,3,1); imagesc(L2); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('L');
subplot(1,3,2); imagesc(a2); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('a');
subplot(1,3,3); imagesc(b2); colormap(gray); axis image off; title('b');

%           NALOGA 2 
% Vpliv posameznih komponent HSV in Lab na končno RGB sliko

clear all; close all; clc;

% Branje slik
img1 = imread('yosemite_meadows.jpg');
img2 = imread('coloured_pencils.jpg');

images = {img1, img2};
names = {'Yosemite', 'Pencils'};


% HSV ANALIZA
for k = 1:2
    img = images{k};
    name = names{k};

    hsvImg = rgb2hsv(img);

    H = hsvImg(:,:,1);
    S = hsvImg(:,:,2);
    V = hsvImg(:,:,3);

    % konstante (polovica maksimalne vrednosti = 0.5)
    constH = 0.5;
    constS = 0.5;
    constV = 0.5;

    % --- 1) Vpliv H
    hsvMod = hsvImg;
    hsvMod(:,:,1) = constH;
    rgbH = hsv2rgb(hsvMod);

    figure('Name',[name ' - HSV brez H']);
    imshow(rgbH); title([name ' - konstanten H']);

    % --- 2) Vpliv S
    hsvMod = hsvImg;
    hsvMod(:,:,2) = constS;
    rgbS = hsv2rgb(hsvMod);

    figure('Name',[name ' - HSV brez S']);
    imshow(rgbS); title([name ' - konstanten S']);

    %% --- 3) Vpliv V
    hsvMod = hsvImg;
    hsvMod(:,:,3) = constV;
    rgbV = hsv2rgb(hsvMod);

    figure('Name',[name ' - HSV brez V']);
    imshow(rgbV); title([name ' - konstanten V']);

end


%       Lab ANALIZA
for k = 1:2
    img = images{k};
    name = names{k};

    labImg = rgb2lab(img);

    L = labImg(:,:,1);
    a = labImg(:,:,2);
    b = labImg(:,:,3);

    % konstante (sredina intervala)
    constL = 50;
    consta = 0;
    constb = 0;

    % --- 1) Vpliv L
    labMod = labImg;
    labMod(:,:,1) = constL;
    rgbL = lab2rgb(labMod);

    figure('Name',[name ' - Lab brez L']);
    imshow(rgbL); title([name ' - konstanten L']);

    % --- 2) Vpliv a
    labMod = labImg;
    labMod(:,:,2) = consta;
    rgbA = lab2rgb(labMod);

    figure('Name',[name ' - Lab brez a']);
    imshow(rgbA); title([name ' - konstanten a']);

    % --- 3) Vpliv b
    labMod = labImg;
    labMod(:,:,3) = constb;
    rgbB = lab2rgb(labMod);

    figure('Name',[name ' - Lab brez b']);
    imshow(rgbB); title([name ' - konstanten b']);

end

==== BEGIN Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_21507_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_3.m ====
clear; close all; clc;


%          NALOGA 3
%  Segmentacija morja z ROI (pravokotnik)

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

% število frejmov
numF = floor(video.FrameRate * video.Duration);

% vzamemo testni frame
frame0 = read(video,50);

figure, imshow(frame0)
title('Označi pravokotnik območja MORJA in 2x klikni');

% izberemo pravokotnik
roi = drawrectangle;
wait(roi);

% izrežemo območje
area = imcrop(frame0, roi.Position);

%   MEJE V RGB (iz pravokotnika)

Rmin = min(area(:,:,1),[],'all');
Rmax = max(area(:,:,1),[],'all');

Gmin = min(area(:,:,2),[],'all');
Gmax = max(area(:,:,2),[],'all');

Bmin = min(area(:,:,3),[],'all');
Bmax = max(area(:,:,3),[],'all');


%   SEGMENTACIJA RGB

outRGB = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_RGB.avi');
outRGB.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outRGB);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video,k);

    mask = frame(:,:,1) >= Rmin & frame(:,:,1) <= Rmax & ...
           frame(:,:,2) >= Gmin & frame(:,:,2) <= Gmax & ...
           frame(:,:,3) >= Bmin & frame(:,:,3) <= Bmax;

    writeVideo(outRGB, uint8(mask)*255);
end

close(outRGB);



%     MEJE V HSV

hsv_area = rgb2hsv(area);

Hmin = min(hsv_area(:,:,1),[],'all');
Hmax = max(hsv_area(:,:,1),[],'all');

Smin = min(hsv_area(:,:,2),[],'all');
Smax = max(hsv_area(:,:,2),[],'all');



%     SEGMENTACIJA HSV

outHSV = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_HSV.avi');
outHSV.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outHSV);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video,k);
    hsv = rgb2hsv(frame);

    % samo H + S
    mask = hsv(:,:,1) >= Hmin & hsv(:,:,1) <= Hmax & ...
           hsv(:,:,2) >= Smin & hsv(:,:,2) <= Smax;

    writeVideo(outHSV, uint8(mask)*255);
end

close(outHSV);
==== END Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_21507_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_3.m ====

clear; close all; clc;


%          NALOGA 3


video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

% število frejmov
numF = floor(video.FrameRate * video.Duration);

% vzamemo testni frame
frame0 = read(video,50);

figure, imshow(frame0)
title('Označi pravokotnik območja MORJA in 2x klikni');

% izberemo pravokotnik
roi = drawrectangle;
wait(roi);

% izrežemo območje
area = imcrop(frame0, roi.Position);


%   MEJE V RGB (iz pravokotnika)

Rmin = min(area(:,:,1),[],'all');
Rmax = max(area(:,:,1),[],'all');

Gmin = min(area(:,:,2),[],'all');
Gmax = max(area(:,:,2),[],'all');

Bmin = min(area(:,:,3),[],'all');
Bmax = max(area(:,:,3),[],'all');


%   SEGMENTACIJA RGB

outRGB = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_RGB.avi');
outRGB.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outRGB);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video,k);

    mask = frame(:,:,1) >= Rmin & frame(:,:,1) <= Rmax & ...
           frame(:,:,2) >= Gmin & frame(:,:,2) <= Gmax & ...
           frame(:,:,3) >= Bmin & frame(:,:,3) <= Bmax;

    writeVideo(outRGB, uint8(mask)*255);
end

close(outRGB);



%     MEJE V HSV

hsv_area = rgb2hsv(area);

Hmin = min(hsv_area(:,:,1),[],'all');
Hmax = max(hsv_area(:,:,1),[],'all');

Smin = min(hsv_area(:,:,2),[],'all');
Smax = max(hsv_area(:,:,2),[],'all');


%     SEGMENTACIJA HSV

outHSV = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_HSV.avi');
outHSV.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outHSV);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video,k);
    hsv = rgb2hsv(frame);

    % samo H + S
    mask = hsv(:,:,1) >= Hmin & hsv(:,:,1) <= Hmax & ...
           hsv(:,:,2) >= Smin & hsv(:,:,2) <= Smax;

    writeVideo(outHSV, uint8(mask)*255);
end

close(outHSV);

==== BEGIN Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_21507_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_5.m ====
clear all; close all; clc;

%     NALOGA 5 
% Histogram Intersection (4x4x4)


% Preberi video
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

% Vzamemo en okvir za učenje (npr. 50)
frame = read(video, 50);
figure; imshow(frame); title('Klikni izsek MORJA (pravokotnik)');
h = imrect;
pos = wait(h);   % uporabnik izbere ROI

% Izreži učni del
x1 = round(pos(1)); 
y1 = round(pos(2));
w  = round(pos(3));
h_ = round(pos(4));

roi = frame(y1:y1+h_-1, x1:x1+w-1, :);   % <-- popravek (w_ ni obstajal!)

figure; imshow(roi); title('Izsek morja za učenje');


%     Izračun 4x4x4 RGB histograma MORJA

bins = 4;
binSize = 256 / bins;

hist_sea = zeros(bins, bins, bins);

for y = 1:size(roi,1)
    for x = 1:size(roi,2)

        R = roi(y,x,1);
        G = roi(y,x,2);
        B = roi(y,x,3);

        r_bin = min(floor(double(R)/binSize) + 1, bins);
        g_bin = min(floor(double(G)/binSize) + 1, bins);
        b_bin = min(floor(double(B)/binSize) + 1, bins);

        hist_sea(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = hist_sea(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;
    end
end

hist_sea = hist_sea / sum(hist_sea(:));  % normalizacija

disp('Histogram MORJA pripravljen.');


%     PRIPRAVA IZHODNEGA VIDEA

out = VideoWriter('ladja_hist_intersection.avi');
out.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(out);


%     OBDELAVA VIDEA

blockSize = 8;

for k = 1:video.NumFrames
    frame = read(video, k);

    % Izhodni sivinski frame
    frameGray = zeros(size(frame,1), size(frame,2), 'uint8');

    % Zanka po blokih
    for y = 1:blockSize:size(frame,1)-blockSize+1
        for x = 1:blockSize:size(frame,2)-blockSize+1

            block = frame(y:y+blockSize-1, x:x+blockSize-1, :);

            % histogram bloka
            hist_block = zeros(bins,bins,bins);

            for yy = 1:blockSize
                for xx = 1:blockSize

                    R = block(yy,xx,1);
                    G = block(yy,xx,2);
                    B = block(yy,xx,3);

                    r_bin = min(floor(double(R)/binSize) + 1, bins);
                    g_bin = min(floor(double(G)/binSize) + 1, bins);
                    b_bin = min(floor(double(B)/binSize) + 1, bins);

                    hist_block(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = ...
                        hist_block(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;
                end
            end

            hist_block = hist_block / sum(hist_block(:));
            %HISTOGRAM INTERSECTION 
            HI = sum(min(hist_block(:), hist_sea(:)));

            val = uint8(HI * 255);

            frameGray(y:y+blockSize-1, x:x+blockSize-1) = val;
        end
    end

    writeVideo(out, frameGray);
    fprintf('Frame %d / %d obdelan.\n', k, video.NumFrames);
end

close(out);
disp('Končano: ladja_hist_intersection.avi');
==== END Adnan Halilović | Adnan Halilović_21507_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_5.m ====

%% Naloga 3
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
outputRGB = VideoWriter('segmented_rgb.avi'); 
outputHSV = VideoWriter('segmented_hsv.avi'); 
open(outputRGB);
open(outputHSV);

% Prikaz prvega okvirja za določanje intervalov
frame = readFrame(video);
imshow(frame);
title('Kliknite na eno točko morja za določitev barvnih intervalov');

% Izbira točke
[x, y] = ginput(1);
x = round(x);
y = round(y);

selectedColor = reshape(frame(y, x, :), 1, 3);

% Določanje intervalov 
tolerance = 30;
minR = max(0, selectedColor(1) - tolerance);
maxR = min(255, selectedColor(1) + tolerance);
minG = max(0, selectedColor(2) - tolerance);
maxG = min(255, selectedColor(2) + tolerance);
minB = max(0, selectedColor(3) - tolerance);
maxB = min(255, selectedColor(3) + tolerance);

disp('Intervali RGB določeni:');
disp(['R: [', num2str(minR), ', ', num2str(maxR), ']']);
disp(['G: [', num2str(minG), ', ', num2str(maxG), ']']);
disp(['B: [', num2str(minB), ', ', num2str(maxB), ']']);

% Pretvorba RGB v HSV in določanje HSV intervalov
selectedColorHSV = rgb2hsv(double(selectedColor) / 255);

minH = max(0, selectedColorHSV(1) - 0.05); 
maxH = min(1, selectedColorHSV(1) + 0.05);
minS = max(0, selectedColorHSV(2) - 0.2); 
maxS = min(1, selectedColorHSV(2) + 0.2);
minV = 0;
maxV = 1;  

disp('Intervali HSV določeni:');
disp(['H: [', num2str(minH), ', ', num2str(maxH), ']']);
disp(['S: [', num2str(minS), ', ', num2str(maxS), ']']);
disp(['V: [', num2str(minV), ', ', num2str(maxV), ']']);

video.CurrentTime = 0;

% Segmentacija videa v RGB prostoru
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);
    
    % RGB segmentacija
    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);
    binaryRGB = (R >= minR & R <= maxR) & ...
                (G >= minG & G <= maxG) & ...
                (B >= minB & B <= maxB);
    
    binaryRGB = uint8(binaryRGB) * 255;
    writeVideo(outputRGB, binaryRGB);
end

video.CurrentTime = 0;

% Segmentacija videa v HSV prostoru
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);
    hsvFrame = rgb2hsv(frame);

    H = hsvFrame(:,:,1);
    S = hsvFrame(:,:,2);
    V = hsvFrame(:,:,3);

    binaryHSV = (H >= minH & H <= maxH) & ...
                (S >= minS & S <= maxS) & ...
                (V >= minV & V <= maxV);

    binaryHSV = uint8(binaryHSV) * 255;
    writeVideo(outputHSV, binaryHSV);
end

close(outputRGB);
close(outputHSV);

%% Naloga 4
videoReader = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frameSample = readFrame(videoReader);
imshow(frameSample);
title('Izberite območje, ki predstavlja tipično barvo morja');

% pravokotno območje
position = round(getrect);
close;

seaRegion = imcrop(frameSample, position);

numBins = 8; % Uporabimo 8x8x8 histogram
binSize = 256 / numBins; % Velikost posameznega intervala

% histogram
hist3D = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins); % Inicializacija 3D histograma

R = seaRegion(:,:,1);
G = seaRegion(:,:,2);
B = seaRegion(:,:,3);
binR = max(ceil(double(R) / binSize), 1); 
binG = max(ceil(double(G) / binSize), 1);
binB = max(ceil(double(B) / binSize), 1);

for i = 1:numel(R)
    hist3D(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) = hist3D(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) + 1;
end

hist3D = hist3D * (255 / max(hist3D(:)));

outputVideo = VideoWriter('morje_sivinska_hist.avi');
open(outputVideo);

videoReader.CurrentTime = 0;

% Procesiramo vsak okvir in ga pretvorimo v sivinsko sliko
while hasFrame(videoReader)
    frame = readFrame(videoReader); % Preberemo trenutni okvir
    
    grayFrame = zeros(size(frame, 1), size(frame, 2), 'uint8');
    
    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);
    
    binR = max(min(ceil(double(R) / binSize), numBins), 1);
    binG = max(min(ceil(double(G) / binSize), numBins), 1);
    binB = max(min(ceil(double(B) / binSize), numBins), 1);
    
    for i = 1:size(frame, 1)
        for j = 1:size(frame, 2)
            grayFrame(i, j) = hist3D(binR(i, j), binG(i, j), binB(i, j));
        end
    end
    
    writeVideo(outputVideo, grayFrame);
end

close(outputVideo);

%% Naloga 5
videoReader = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

frameSample = readFrame(videoReader);
imshow(frameSample);
title('Izberite območje, ki predstavlja tipično barvo morja');

position = round(getrect);
close; 
seaRegion = imcrop(frameSample, position);

numBins = 4; % 4x4x4 histogram
binSize = 256 / numBins;

seaHist = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins); % Inicializacija 3D histograma za morje

R = seaRegion(:,:,1);
G = seaRegion(:,:,2);
B = seaRegion(:,:,3);
binR = max(ceil(double(R) / binSize), 1); 
binG = max(ceil(double(G) / binSize), 1);
binB = max(ceil(double(B) / binSize), 1);

for i = 1:numel(R)
    seaHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) = seaHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) + 1;
end

seaHist = seaHist / sum(seaHist(:));

outputVideo = VideoWriter('morje_histogram_intersection.avi');
open(outputVideo);

videoReader.CurrentTime = 0;


blockSize = 8;

while hasFrame(videoReader)
    frame = readFrame(videoReader); 
    
    grayFrame = zeros(size(frame, 1), size(frame, 2), 'uint8');
    
    for row = 1:blockSize:size(frame, 1) - blockSize + 1
        for col = 1:blockSize:size(frame, 2) - blockSize + 1
           
            block = frame(row:row+blockSize-1, col:col+blockSize-1, :);
            
          
            blockHist = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins);
            
      
            R = block(:,:,1);
            G = block(:,:,2);
            B = block(:,:,3);
            
            binR = max(ceil(double(R) / binSize), 1); 
            binG = max(ceil(double(G) / binSize), 1);
            binB = max(ceil(double(B) / binSize), 1);
            
      
            for i = 1:numel(R)
                blockHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) = blockHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) + 1;
            end
            
      
            blockHist = blockHist / sum(blockHist(:));
            

            intersectionValue = sum(min(blockHist(:), seaHist(:))) * 256; 
            
   
            grayFrame(row:row+blockSize-1, col:col+blockSize-1) = uint8(intersectionValue);
        end
    end
    
 
    writeVideo(outputVideo, grayFrame);
end

close(outputVideo);

clear all; close all; clc;
%% ===============================
%          NALOGA 2
%  Vpliv posameznih komponent HSV in Lab na končno RGB sliko
% ================================

%% Branje slik
img1 = imread('yosemite_meadows.jpg');
img2 = imread('coloured_pencils.jpg');

images = {img1, img2};
names = {'Yosemite', 'Pencils'};


%% HSV ANALIZA 
for k = 1:2
    img = images{k};
    name = names{k};

    hsvImg = rgb2hsv(img);

    % KONSTANTE
    constH = 0.5;
    constS = 0.5;
    constV = 0.5;

    % 1) Konstanten H
    hsvMod = hsvImg;
    hsvMod(:,:,1) = constH;
    rgbH = hsv2rgb(hsvMod);

    % 2) Konstanten S
    hsvMod = hsvImg;
    hsvMod(:,:,2) = constS;
    rgbS = hsv2rgb(hsvMod);

    % 3) Konstanten V
    hsvMod = hsvImg;
    hsvMod(:,:,3) = constV;
    rgbV = hsv2rgb(hsvMod);

    % ---- RISANJE NA ENO FIGURO ----
    figure('Name',[name ' - HSV vpliv komponent']);
    sgtitle([name ' – vpliv konstantnega H, S in V']);

    subplot(1,3,1); imshow(rgbH); title('Konstanten H');
    subplot(1,3,2); imshow(rgbS); title('Konstanten S');
    subplot(1,3,3); imshow(rgbV); title('Konstanten V');
end


%%  Lab ANALIZA 
for k = 1:2
    img = images{k};
    name = names{k};

    labImg = rgb2lab(img);

    % KONSTANTE
    constL = 50;
    consta = 0;
    constb = 50;

    % 1) Konstanten L
    labMod = labImg;
    labMod(:,:,1) = constL;
    rgbL = lab2rgb(labMod);

    % 2) Konstanten a
    labMod = labImg;
    labMod(:,:,2) = consta;
    rgbA = lab2rgb(labMod);

    % 3) Konstanten b
    labMod = labImg;
    labMod(:,:,3) = constb;
    rgbB = lab2rgb(labMod);

    % ---- RISANJE NA ENO FIGURO ----
    figure('Name',[name ' - Lab vpliv komponent']);
    sgtitle([name ' – vpliv konstantnega L, a in b']);

    subplot(1,3,1); imshow(rgbL); title('Konstanten L');
    subplot(1,3,2); imshow(rgbA); title('Konstanten a');
    subplot(1,3,3); imshow(rgbB); title('Konstanten b');
end

clear; close all; clc;

%% ===============================
%          NALOGA 3
%  Segmentacija morja
% ===============================

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

% število frejmov
numF = floor(video.FrameRate * video.Duration);

% vzamemo testni frame
frame0 = read(video,50);

figure, imshow(frame0)
title('Označi pravokotnik območja MORJA in 2x klikni');

% izberemo pravokotnik
roi = drawrectangle;
wait(roi);

% izrežemo območje
area = imcrop(frame0, roi.Position);

%% ===============================
%   MEJE V RGB (iz pravokotnika)
%% ===============================

Rmin = min(area(:,:,1),[],'all');
Rmax = max(area(:,:,1),[],'all');

Gmin = min(area(:,:,2),[],'all');
Gmax = max(area(:,:,2),[],'all');

Bmin = min(area(:,:,3),[],'all');
Bmax = max(area(:,:,3),[],'all');

%   SEGMENTACIJA RGB


outRGB = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_RGB.avi');
outRGB.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outRGB);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video,k);

    mask = frame(:,:,1) >= Rmin & frame(:,:,1) <= Rmax & ...
           frame(:,:,2) >= Gmin & frame(:,:,2) <= Gmax & ...
           frame(:,:,3) >= Bmin & frame(:,:,3) <= Bmax;

    writeVideo(outRGB, uint8(mask)*255);
end

close(outRGB);



%     MEJE V HSV


hsv_area = rgb2hsv(area);

Hmin = min(hsv_area(:,:,1),[],'all');
Hmax = max(hsv_area(:,:,1),[],'all');

Smin = min(hsv_area(:,:,2),[],'all');
Smax = max(hsv_area(:,:,2),[],'all');


%     SEGMENTACIJA HSV


outHSV = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_HSV.avi');
outHSV.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outHSV);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video,k);
    hsv = rgb2hsv(frame);

    % samo H + S
    mask = hsv(:,:,1) >= Hmin & hsv(:,:,1) <= Hmax & ...
           hsv(:,:,2) >= Smin & hsv(:,:,2) <= Smax;

    writeVideo(outHSV, uint8(mask)*255);
end

close(outHSV);

==== BEGIN Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_21501_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_4.m ====
clear all; close all; clc;

%% ===============================
%      NALOGA 4 – 3D RGB HISTOGRAM
% ================================

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
numF = floor(video.FrameRate * video.Duration);

%% --- 1. OKVIR IN IZREŽEMO MORJE ---
frame = read(video, 50);  % isti kot prej

figure; imshow(frame); title('Označi pravokotnik z MORJEM (tipično območje)');
rect = round(getrect());   % uporabnik z miško označi ROI

% izrez
roi = imcrop(frame, rect);

figure; imshow(roi); title('Izrez morja');

%% --- 2. KONSTRUKCIJA 3D HISTOGRAMA (8 × 8 × 8) ---

bins = 8;              % št. binov po osi R, G, B
binSize = 256 / bins;  % velikost posameznega intervala

% 3D histogram
H = zeros(bins, bins, bins);

% napolnimo histogram iz ROI
for y = 1:size(roi,1)
    for x = 1:size(roi,2)

        R = roi(y,x,1);
        G = roi(y,x,2);
        B = roi(y,x,3);

        % izračun bin indeksa
        Rbin = floor(double(R) / binSize) + 1;
        Gbin = floor(double(G) / binSize) + 1;
        Bbin = floor(double(B) / binSize) + 1;

        % omejitev (če je R==255 → pade v zadnji bin)
        Rbin = min(Rbin, bins);
        Gbin = min(Gbin, bins);
        Bbin = min(Bbin, bins);

        H(Rbin, Gbin, Bbin) = H(Rbin, Gbin, Bbin) + 1;
    end
end

%% --- 3. NORMALIZACIJA HISTOGRAMA (max = 255) ---

H = H * (255 / max(H(:)));


%% --- 4. SEGMENTACIJA VIDEA S POMOČJO HISTOGRAMA ---

out = VideoWriter('ladja_segment_histogram.avi');
out.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(out);

for k = 1:numF
    frame = read(video, k);

    % izhodna slika (sivinska)
    outFrame = zeros(size(frame,1), size(frame,2), 'uint8');

    for y = 1:size(frame,1)
        for x = 1:size(frame,2)

            R = frame(y,x,1);
            G = frame(y,x,2);
            B = frame(y,x,3);

            Rbin = floor(double(R) / binSize) + 1;
            Gbin = floor(double(G) / binSize) + 1;
            Bbin = floor(double(B) / binSize) + 1;

            Rbin = min(Rbin, bins);
            Gbin = min(Gbin, bins);
            Bbin = min(Bbin, bins);

            % sivinska vrednost = vrednost histograma tega 3D bin-a
            outFrame(y,x) = uint8(H(Rbin, Gbin, Bbin));
        end
    end

    % VideoWriter zahteva 3-kanalno sliko, zato sivinsko ponovimo v RGB
    rgbFrame = repmat(outFrame, [1 1 3]);

    writeVideo(out, rgbFrame);
end

close(out);

==== END Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_21501_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_4.m ====

==== BEGIN Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_21501_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_5.m ====
clear all; close all; clc;

%% ===============================================
%     NALOGA 5 – Histogram Intersection (4x4x4)
% ================================================

% Preberi video
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

% Vzamemo en okvir za učenje (npr. 50)
frame = read(video, 50);
figure; imshow(frame); title('Klikni izsek MORJA (pravokotnik)');
h = imrect;
pos = wait(h);   % uporabnik izbere ROI

% Izreži učni del
x1 = round(pos(1)); 
y1 = round(pos(2));
w  = round(pos(3));
h_ = round(pos(4));

roi = frame(y1:y1+h_-1, x1:x1+w-1, :);   % <-- popravek (w_ ni obstajal!)

figure; imshow(roi); title('Izsek morja za učenje');

%% ===============================================
%     Izračun 4x4x4 RGB histograma MORJA
% ================================================

bins = 4;
binSize = 256 / bins;

hist_sea = zeros(bins, bins, bins);

for y = 1:size(roi,1)
    for x = 1:size(roi,2)

        R = roi(y,x,1);
        G = roi(y,x,2);
        B = roi(y,x,3);

        r_bin = min(floor(double(R)/binSize) + 1, bins);
        g_bin = min(floor(double(G)/binSize) + 1, bins);
        b_bin = min(floor(double(B)/binSize) + 1, bins);

        hist_sea(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = hist_sea(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;
    end
end

hist_sea = hist_sea / sum(hist_sea(:));  % normalizacija

disp('Histogram MORJA pripravljen.');

%     PRIPRAVA IZHODNEGA VIDEA


out = VideoWriter('ladja_hist_intersection.avi');
out.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(out);


%     OBDELAVA VIDEA


blockSize = 8;

for k = 1:video.NumFrames
    frame = read(video, k);

    % Izhodni sivinski frame
    frameGray = zeros(size(frame,1), size(frame,2), 'uint8');

    % Zanka po blokih
    for y = 1:blockSize:size(frame,1)-blockSize+1
        for x = 1:blockSize:size(frame,2)-blockSize+1

            block = frame(y:y+blockSize-1, x:x+blockSize-1, :);

            % histogram bloka
            hist_block = zeros(bins,bins,bins);

            for yy = 1:blockSize
                for xx = 1:blockSize

                    R = block(yy,xx,1);
                    G = block(yy,xx,2);
                    B = block(yy,xx,3);

                    r_bin = min(floor(double(R)/binSize) + 1, bins);
                    g_bin = min(floor(double(G)/binSize) + 1, bins);
                    b_bin = min(floor(double(B)/binSize) + 1, bins);

                    hist_block(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = ...
                        hist_block(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;
                end
            end

            hist_block = hist_block / sum(hist_block(:));

         
            HI = sum(min(hist_block(:), hist_sea(:)));

            val = uint8(HI * 255);

            frameGray(y:y+blockSize-1, x:x+blockSize-1) = val;
        end
    end

    writeVideo(out, frameGray);
    fprintf('Frame %d / %d obdelan.\n', k, video.NumFrames);
end

close(out);

==== END Alja Belinger | Alja Belinger_21501_assignsubmission_file | RV_3_5.m ====

%% Naloga 3
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
outputRGB = VideoWriter('segmented_rgb.avi'); 
outputHSV = VideoWriter('segmented_hsv.avi'); 
open(outputRGB);
open(outputHSV);

% Prikaz prvega okvirja za določanje intervalov
frame = readFrame(video);
imshow(frame);
title('Kliknite na eno točko morja za določitev barvnih intervalov');

% Izbira točke
[x, y] = ginput(1);
x = round(x);
y = round(y);

selectedColor = reshape(frame(y, x, :), 1, 3);

% Določanje intervalov 
tolerance = 30;
minR = max(0, selectedColor(1) - tolerance);
maxR = min(255, selectedColor(1) + tolerance);
minG = max(0, selectedColor(2) - tolerance);
maxG = min(255, selectedColor(2) + tolerance);
minB = max(0, selectedColor(3) - tolerance);
maxB = min(255, selectedColor(3) + tolerance);

disp('Intervali RGB določeni:');
disp(['R: [', num2str(minR), ', ', num2str(maxR), ']']);
disp(['G: [', num2str(minG), ', ', num2str(maxG), ']']);
disp(['B: [', num2str(minB), ', ', num2str(maxB), ']']);

% Pretvorba RGB v HSV in določanje HSV intervalov
selectedColorHSV = rgb2hsv(double(selectedColor) / 255);

minH = max(0, selectedColorHSV(1) - 0.05); 
maxH = min(1, selectedColorHSV(1) + 0.05);
minS = max(0, selectedColorHSV(2) - 0.2); 
maxS = min(1, selectedColorHSV(2) + 0.2);
minV = 0;
maxV = 1;  

disp('Intervali HSV določeni:');
disp(['H: [', num2str(minH), ', ', num2str(maxH), ']']);
disp(['S: [', num2str(minS), ', ', num2str(maxS), ']']);
disp(['V: [', num2str(minV), ', ', num2str(maxV), ']']);

video.CurrentTime = 0;

% Segmentacija videa v RGB prostoru
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);
    
    % RGB segmentacija
    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);
    binaryRGB = (R >= minR & R <= maxR) & ...
                (G >= minG & G <= maxG) & ...
                (B >= minB & B <= maxB);
    
    binaryRGB = uint8(binaryRGB) * 255;
    writeVideo(outputRGB, binaryRGB);
end

video.CurrentTime = 0;

% Segmentacija videa v HSV prostoru
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);
    hsvFrame = rgb2hsv(frame);

    H = hsvFrame(:,:,1);
    S = hsvFrame(:,:,2);
    V = hsvFrame(:,:,3);

    binaryHSV = (H >= minH & H <= maxH) & ...
                (S >= minS & S <= maxS) & ...
                (V >= minV & V <= maxV);

    binaryHSV = uint8(binaryHSV) * 255;
    writeVideo(outputHSV, binaryHSV);
end

close(outputRGB);
close(outputHSV);

%% Naloga 4
videoReader = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frameSample = readFrame(videoReader);
imshow(frameSample);
title('Izberite območje, ki predstavlja tipično barvo morja');

% pravokotno območje
position = round(getrect);
close;

seaRegion = imcrop(frameSample, position);

numBins = 8; % Uporabimo 8x8x8 histogram
binSize = 256 / numBins; % Velikost posameznega intervala

% histogram
hist3D = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins); % Inicializacija 3D histograma

R = seaRegion(:,:,1);
G = seaRegion(:,:,2);
B = seaRegion(:,:,3);
binR = max(ceil(double(R) / binSize), 1); 
binG = max(ceil(double(G) / binSize), 1);
binB = max(ceil(double(B) / binSize), 1);

for i = 1:numel(R)
    hist3D(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) = hist3D(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) + 1;
end

hist3D = hist3D * (255 / max(hist3D(:)));

outputVideo = VideoWriter('morje_sivinska_hist.avi');
open(outputVideo);

videoReader.CurrentTime = 0;

% Procesiramo vsak okvir in ga pretvorimo v sivinsko sliko
while hasFrame(videoReader)
    frame = readFrame(videoReader); % Preberemo trenutni okvir
    
    grayFrame = zeros(size(frame, 1), size(frame, 2), 'uint8');
    
    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);
    
    binR = max(min(ceil(double(R) / binSize), numBins), 1);
    binG = max(min(ceil(double(G) / binSize), numBins), 1);
    binB = max(min(ceil(double(B) / binSize), numBins), 1);
    
    for i = 1:size(frame, 1)
        for j = 1:size(frame, 2)
            grayFrame(i, j) = hist3D(binR(i, j), binG(i, j), binB(i, j));
        end
    end
    
    writeVideo(outputVideo, grayFrame);
end

close(outputVideo);

%% Naloga 5
videoReader = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

frameSample = readFrame(videoReader);
imshow(frameSample);
title('Izberite območje, ki predstavlja tipično barvo morja');

position = round(getrect);
close; 
seaRegion = imcrop(frameSample, position);

numBins = 4; % 4x4x4 histogram
binSize = 256 / numBins;

seaHist = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins); % Inicializacija 3D histograma za morje

R = seaRegion(:,:,1);
G = seaRegion(:,:,2);
B = seaRegion(:,:,3);
binR = max(ceil(double(R) / binSize), 1); 
binG = max(ceil(double(G) / binSize), 1);
binB = max(ceil(double(B) / binSize), 1);

for i = 1:numel(R)
    seaHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) = seaHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) + 1;
end

seaHist = seaHist / sum(seaHist(:));

outputVideo = VideoWriter('morje_histogram_intersection.avi');
open(outputVideo);

videoReader.CurrentTime = 0;


blockSize = 8;

while hasFrame(videoReader)
    frame = readFrame(videoReader); 
    
    grayFrame = zeros(size(frame, 1), size(frame, 2), 'uint8');
    
    for row = 1:blockSize:size(frame, 1) - blockSize + 1
        for col = 1:blockSize:size(frame, 2) - blockSize + 1
           
            block = frame(row:row+blockSize-1, col:col+blockSize-1, :);
            
          
            blockHist = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins);
            
      
            R = block(:,:,1);
            G = block(:,:,2);
            B = block(:,:,3);
            
            binR = max(ceil(double(R) / binSize), 1); 
            binG = max(ceil(double(G) / binSize), 1);
            binB = max(ceil(double(B) / binSize), 1);
            
      
            for i = 1:numel(R)
                blockHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) = blockHist(binR(i), binG(i), binB(i)) + 1;
            end
            
      
            blockHist = blockHist / sum(blockHist(:));
            

            intersectionValue = sum(min(blockHist(:), seaHist(:)))/sum(seaHist(:)) * 256;
            
   
            grayFrame(row:row+blockSize-1, col:col+blockSize-1) = uint8(intersectionValue);
        end
    end
    
 
    writeVideo(outputVideo, grayFrame);
end

close(outputVideo);

%% RGB

vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(vid, [1 Inf]);

% Dimenzije videa
[H, W, ~, N] = size(frames);

% Izgled 50. framea (Opcionalno)
%frame_50 = frames(:, :, :, 50);

for m = 1:N
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W

            R = frames(i, j, 1, m);
            G = frames(i, j, 2, m);
            B = frames(i, j, 3, m);

            %odredjeno iz koda za min i max vrijednosti
            if (33 <= R && R <= 114 && 63 <= G && G <= 139 && 37 <= B && B <= 129)
                frames(i, j, 1, m) = 255;
                frames(i, j, 2, m) = 255;
                frames(i, j, 3, m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i, j, 1, m) = 0;
                frames(i, j, 2, m) = 0;
                frames(i, j, 3, m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

writer_object = VideoWriter('ladjaRGB.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = 25;
open(writer_object);
writeVideo(writer_object, frames);
close(writer_object);

%% HSV1
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(vid, [1 Inf]);

% dimenzije
[H, W, ~, nFrames] = size(frames);

% konverzija svih frameova u HSV
framesHSV = zeros(H, W, 3, nFrames);
for m = 1:nFrames
    framesHSV(:,:,:,m) = rgb2hsv(frames(:,:,:,m));
end

%min i max za H
Hmin = 0.35;
Hmax = 0.45;

for m = 1:nFrames
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W
            if Hmin <= framesHSV(i,j,1,m) && framesHSV(i,j,1,m) <= Hmax
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

% zapis videa
writer_object = VideoWriter('ladjaHSV1.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = 25;
open(writer_object);
writeVideo(writer_object, frames);
close(writer_object);

%% HSV2

% Učitaj sve frameove videa
ladja  = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(ladja,[1 Inf]);
[H, W, ~, nFrames] = size(frames);

% svi rameovi u HSV
framesHSV = zeros(H, W, 3, nFrames);
for k = 1:nFrames
    framesHSV(:,:,:,k) = rgb2hsv(frames(:,:,:,k));
end

% H i S, min i max
Hmin2 = 0.35;  
Hmax2 = 0.45;
Smin2 = 0.2;  
Smax2 = 0.45;

for m = 1:nFrames
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W
            Hval = framesHSV(i,j,1,m);
            Vval = framesHSV(i,j,3,m);

            if (Hval >= Hmin2 && Hval <= Hmax2 && Vval >= Smin2 && Vval <= Smax2)
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

% rezultat
writer_object = VideoWriter('ladjaHSV2.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = 25;
open(writer_object);
writeVideo(writer_object, frames);
close(writer_object);

%% HSV3

ladja   = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames  = read(ladja, [1 Inf]);
nFrames = size(frames, 4);

% Konverzija svih frameova u HSV
framesHSV = zeros(H,W,3,nFrames);
for m = 1:nFrames

end

Hmin3 = 0.35;  
Hmax3 = 0.45;
Smin3 = 0.2;  
Smax3 = 0.45;
Vmin = 0.3;  
Vmax = 0.49;

% piksel je bijel ako zadovoljava sva tri uslova, inače crn
[H, W, ~, ~] = size(frames);
for m = 1:nFrames
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W
            Hval = framesHSV(i,j,1,m);
            Sval = framesHSV(i,j,2,m);
            Vval = framesHSV(i,j,3,m);

            if (Hval >= Hmin3 && Hval <= Hmax3 && Sval >= Smin3 && Sval <= Smax3 && Vval >= Vmin && Vval <= Vmax)
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

%Jedan frame iz videa
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(vid, 50);

figure; imshow(frame);
title('Klikni 15 tocaka na moru');

[x, y] = ginput(15);

%Očitane RGB vrijednosti
vals = zeros(length(x), 3);

%Očitan RGB za svaku točku
for i = 1:length(x)
    xi = round(x(i));
    yi = round(y(i));
    vals(i, :) = frame(yi, xi, :);
end

%min i max
Rmin = min(vals(:,1));
Rmax = max(vals(:,1));
Gmin = min(vals(:,2));
Gmax = max(vals(:,2));
Bmin = min(vals(:,3));
Bmax = max(vals(:,3));

%Rezultati
fprintf('Rmin = %d , Rmax = %d\n', Rmin, Rmax);
fprintf('Gmin = %d , Gmax = %d\n', Gmin, Gmax);
fprintf('Bmin = %d , Bmax = %d\n', Bmin, Bmax);

==== BEGIN Azra Bulić | Azra Bulić_21515_assignsubmission_file | naloga4.m ====
%% NALOGA 4: Avtomatska segmentacija morja z 3D RGB histogramom
clear; close all; clc;

%% Naloži video
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

%%  Preberi prvi frame za učno območje
frame = read(video, 1);
imshow(frame); hold on;
title('Klikni vsaj 3 točke poligona za označitev morja, Enter ko končaš');

%%  Interaktivni poligon za morje
[x, y] = ginput;  % klikni več točk in pritisni Enter

% Preveri, da je vsaj 3 točke
if length(x) < 3
    error('Moraš klikniti vsaj 3 točke za poligon.');
end

% Prikaži poligon
plot([x; x(1)], [y; y(1)], 'r-', 'LineWidth', 2);

%% Ustvarjanje maske z inpolygon (brez poly2mask)
[XX, YY] = meshgrid(1:size(frame,2), 1:size(frame,1));
mask = inpolygon(XX, YY, x, y);

% Prikaz maske
figure;
imshow(mask);
title('Maska izbranega območja');

%%Ekstrakcija pikslov morja
R_sel = double(frame(:,:,1));
G_sel = double(frame(:,:,2));
B_sel = double(frame(:,:,3));

R_sel = R_sel(mask);
G_sel = G_sel(mask);
B_sel = B_sel(mask);

%% Ustvarjanje 3D RGB histograma
numBins = 8;                        % 8x8x8 binov
edges = linspace(0, 255, numBins+1);
hist3D = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins);

for i = 1:length(R_sel)
    r_bin = find(edges >= R_sel(i),1)-1; r_bin = max(r_bin,1);
    g_bin = find(edges >= G_sel(i),1)-1; g_bin = max(g_bin,1);
    b_bin = find(edges >= B_sel(i),1)-1; b_bin = max(b_bin,1);
    hist3D(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) = hist3D(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) + 1;
end

% Normalizacija histograma (maksimalni bin = 255)
hist3D = uint8(255 * hist3D / max(hist3D(:)));

outVid = VideoWriter('naloga4_output.avi');
outVid.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outVid);

%%  Obdelava vseh frame-ov videa (vektorizirano)
video.CurrentTime = 0;
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);

    Rf = double(frame(:,:,1));
    Gf = double(frame(:,:,2));6
    Bf = double(frame(:,:,3));

    % določitev binov
    r_bin = discretize(Rf, edges);
    g_bin = discretize(Gf, edges);
    b_bin = discretize(Bf, edges);

    % nadomestimo NaN z 1
    r_bin(isnan(r_bin)) = 1;
    g_bin(isnan(g_bin)) = 1;
    b_bin(isnan(b_bin)) = 1;

    % vektorizirana dodelitev sivinskega frame-a
    grayFrame = hist3D(sub2ind(size(hist3D), r_bin, g_bin, b_bin));
    grayFrame = uint8(grayFrame);

    writeVideo(outVid, grayFrame);
end

close(outVid);
disp('Video naloge 4 ustvarjen: naloga4_output.avi');
==== END Azra Bulić | Azra Bulić_21515_assignsubmission_file | naloga4.m ====

==== BEGIN Azra Bulić | Azra Bulić_21515_assignsubmission_file | naloga5.m ====
%% NALOGA 5: Segmentacija z histogram intersection
clear; close all; clc;

%% 1) Naloži video
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video,1);

%% 2) Interaktivni poligon za morje
imshow(frame); hold on;
title('Klikni vsaj 3 točke poligona za morje, Enter ko končaš');
[x, y] = ginput;
if length(x) < 3
    error('Moraš klikniti vsaj 3 točke za poligon.');
end
plot([x;x(1)],[y;y(1)],'r-','LineWidth',2);

[XX, YY] = meshgrid(1:size(frame,2), 1:size(frame,1));
mask = inpolygon(XX, YY, x, y);

%% 3) Histogram morja 4x4x4
numBins = 4;
edges = linspace(0,255,numBins+1);

R_sel = double(frame(:,:,1)); R_sel = R_sel(mask);
G_sel = double(frame(:,:,2)); G_sel = G_sel(mask);
B_sel = double(frame(:,:,3)); B_sel = B_sel(mask);

histSea = zeros(numBins,numBins,numBins);
for i = 1:length(R_sel)
    r_bin = find(edges >= R_sel(i),1)-1; r_bin = max(r_bin,1);
    g_bin = find(edges >= G_sel(i),1)-1; g_bin = max(g_bin,1);
    b_bin = find(edges >= B_sel(i),1)-1; b_bin = max(b_bin,1);
    histSea(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) = histSea(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) + 1;
end
histSea = histSea / sum(histSea(:)); % normaliziramo na vsoto 1

%% 4) Priprava output videa
outVid = VideoWriter('naloga5_output.avi');
outVid.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outVid);

blockSize = 8;  % 8x8 pikslov

%% 5) Obdelava frame-ov
video.CurrentTime = 0;
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);
    [H, W, ~] = size(frame);
    
    % Število blokov
    H_blocks = floor(H/blockSize);
    W_blocks = floor(W/blockSize);
    
    grayFrame = zeros(H_blocks*blockSize, W_blocks*blockSize, 'uint8');
    
    % Obdelaj vsak blok
    for i = 1:H_blocks
        for j = 1:W_blocks
            rowIdx = (i-1)*blockSize + (1:blockSize);
            colIdx = (j-1)*blockSize + (1:blockSize);
            
            R_block = double(frame(rowIdx, colIdx,1));
            G_block = double(frame(rowIdx, colIdx,2));
            B_block = double(frame(rowIdx, colIdx,3));
            
            % histogram bloka
            histBlock = zeros(numBins,numBins,numBins);
            for r = 1:blockSize
                for c = 1:blockSize
                    r_bin = find(edges >= R_block(r,c),1)-1; r_bin = max(r_bin,1);
                    g_bin = find(edges >= G_block(r,c),1)-1; g_bin = max(g_bin,1);
                    b_bin = find(edges >= B_block(r,c),1)-1; b_bin = max(b_bin,1);
                    histBlock(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) = histBlock(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) + 1;
                end
            end
            histBlock = histBlock / sum(histBlock(:));
            
            % Histogram intersection
           intersectVal = sum(min(histSea(:), histBlock(:))) / sum(histSea(:));
            
            % Shrani v sivinski blok (0-256)
            grayBlock = uint8(intersectVal*256);
            grayFrame(rowIdx, colIdx) = grayBlock;
        end
    end
    
    writeVideo(outVid, grayFrame);
end

close(outVid);
disp('Video naloge 5 ustvarjen: naloga5_output.avi');
==== END Azra Bulić | Azra Bulić_21515_assignsubmission_file | naloga5.m ====

           G >= Gmin & G <= Gmax & ...

%Naloga 3
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video, video.NumFrames);  % uporabi zadnji okvir
%imshow(frame)
%title('Izberi območje morja')
out_H = VideoWriter('MorjeHistogram.avi');
%title("izberi odsek morja")
crop = imcrop(frame);

nBins = 8;
crop = im2double(crop); % [0,1]
R = crop(:,:,1);
G = crop(:,:,2);
B = crop(:,:,3);

% Indeksi binov za ROI
r_idx = ceil(R * nBins);
g_idx = ceil(G * nBins);
b_idx = ceil(B * nBins);

r_idx(r_idx==0) = 1;
g_idx(g_idx==0) = 1;
b_idx(b_idx==0) = 1;

hist3D = zeros(nBins, nBins, nBins);
for i = 1:numel(R)
    r = r_idx(i);
    g = g_idx(i);
    b = b_idx(i);
    hist3D(r,g,b) = hist3D(r,g,b) + 1;
end

% normalizacija: max bin -> 255
hist = hist3D / max(hist3D(:)) * 255;   % double, [0,255]

open(out_H);

for t = 1:video.NumFrames
    frame = read(video,t);
    frame = im2double(frame);

    % Indeksi binov za CEL frame
    r_indx = ceil(frame(:,:,1) * nBins);
    g_indx = ceil(frame(:,:,2) * nBins);
    b_indx = ceil(frame(:,:,3) * nBins);

    r_indx(r_indx == 0) = 1;
    g_indx(g_indx == 0) = 1;
    b_indx(b_indx == 0) = 1;

    outFrame = zeros(size(frame,1), size(frame,2));

    % za vsak piksel: vzemi vrednost pripadajočega bina
    for i = 1:numel(outFrame)
        outFrame(i) = hist(r_indx(i), g_indx(i), b_indx(i));
    end

    % v video zapišemo uint8 sivinsko sliko
    writeVideo(out_H, uint8(outFrame));
end

close(out_H);
%%
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video, video.NumFrames);  % uporabi zadnji okvir
imshow(frame)
title('Izberi območje morja')
out_H = VideoWriter('MorjeHistogram.avi');

crop = imcrop(frame);
title("izberi odsek morja")
nBins = 4;
crop = im2double(crop); % [0,1]
R = crop(:,:,1);
G = crop(:,:,2);
B = crop(:,:,3);

% Indeksi binov za ROI
r_idx = ceil(R * nBins);
g_idx = ceil(G * nBins);
b_idx = ceil(B * nBins);

r_idx(r_idx==0) = 1;
g_idx(g_idx==0) = 1;
b_idx(b_idx==0) = 1;

hist3D = zeros(nBins, nBins, nBins);
for i = 1:numel(R)
    r = r_idx(i);
    g = g_idx(i);
    b = b_idx(i);
    hist3D(r,g,b) = hist3D(r,g,b) + 1;
end

% normalizacija: max bin -> 255
hist = hist3D / max(hist3D(:)) * 255;   % double, [0,255]
histSea = hist/sum(hist(:));

blockSize = 8;

out_Hintersection = VideoWriter('Morje_HistIntersection.avi');
open(out_Hintersection);

for t = 1:video.NumFrames
    frame = read(video, t);
    frame = im2double(frame);
    [rows, cols, ~] = size(frame);
    
    outFrame = zeros(rows, cols); % rezultat (svetlost glede na podobnost)
    
    for r = 1:blockSize:rows - mod(rows, blockSize)
        for c = 1:blockSize:cols - mod(cols, blockSize)
            
            % Izreži blok 8×8
            block = frame(r:r+blockSize-1, c:c+blockSize-1, :);
            R = block(:,:,1); G = block(:,:,2); B = block(:,:,3);

            % Histogram bloka
            r_idx = ceil(R * nBins);
            g_idx = ceil(G * nBins);
            b_idx = ceil(B * nBins);

            r_idx(r_idx < 1) = 1; r_idx(r_idx > nBins) = nBins;
            g_idx(g_idx < 1) = 1; g_idx(g_idx > nBins) = nBins;
            b_idx(b_idx < 1) = 1; b_idx(b_idx > nBins) = nBins;

            histBlock = zeros(nBins, nBins, nBins);
            for i = 1:numel(R)
                histBlock(r_idx(i), g_idx(i), b_idx(i)) = histBlock(r_idx(i), g_idx(i), b_idx(i)) + 1;
            end
            %histBlock = histBlock / sum(histSea(:));

            % Izračun preseka histogramov (histogram intersection)
            Presek = sum(min(hist(:), histBlock(:)))/sum(histBlock(:));

            % Pretvori podobnost v 0–255 in shrani v izhodni okvir
            outFrame(r:r+blockSize-1, c:c+blockSize-1) = Presek * 256;
        end
    end
    
    % Pretvori v uint8 in zapiši
    outFrame = uint8(outFrame);
    writeVideo(out_Hintersection, outFrame);
end

close(out_Hintersection);

barvice = imread("coloured_pencils.jpg");

barvice_HSV = rgb2hsv(barvice);

%% 4. 3D histogram

ladja = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frame = read(ladja, 5);

% morje
% x: 0 -> do konca
% y: 285 -> do konca

morje = frame(320:height(frame), 1:width(frame),:);
%figure;
%imshow(morje);

locljivost = 8;
histogram3d = zeros(locljivost,locljivost,locljivost);

n_na_bin = 256/locljivost;

morje = double(morje);
for col = 1:height(morje)
    for row = 1:width(morje)
        
        R = morje(col,row,1);
        G = morje(col,row,2);
        B = morje(col,row,3);
        
        binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;
        
        histogram3d(binR, binG, binB) = histogram3d(binR, binG, binB)+1; % dodamo na pravo mesto
    end
end

maxValue = max(histogram3d(:));
% vrednost najmocnejsega bina == 255
hist_norm = uint8( (double(histogram3d) / double(maxValue)) * 255 );


hist_segmentacija = VideoWriter("ladja_mask_hist.avi");
hist_segmentacija.FrameRate = ladja.FrameRate;   % enak fps kot original
open(hist_segmentacija);


while hasFrame(ladja)
    frame = double(readFrame(ladja));

    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);
    % matrike, kje ima vsak piksel vrednost v kater bin pade v kanalu
    binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
    binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
    binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;

        
    [h, w] = size(R);
    gray = zeros(h, w, 'uint8');
    % piksel v rezultatu (sivinska slika) je vrednost iz histograma 
    for i = 1:h
        for j = 1:w
            gray(i,j) = hist_norm( binR(i,j), binG(i,j), binB(i,j) );
        end
    end

    % 3 kanali
    grayRGB = cat(3, gray, gray, gray);

    writeVideo(hist_segmentacija, grayRGB);
end

close(hist_segmentacija);


%% 5. 3D histogram

ladja = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frame = read(ladja, 5);

% morje
% x: 0 -> do konca
% y: 285 -> do konca

morje = frame(320:height(frame), 1:width(frame),:);
%figure;
%imshow(morje);

locljivost = 4;
histogram3d = zeros(locljivost,locljivost,locljivost);

n_na_bin = 256/locljivost;

morje = double(morje);
for col = 1:height(morje)
    for row = 1:width(morje)
        
        R = morje(col,row,1);
        G = morje(col,row,2);
        B = morje(col,row,3);
        
        binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;
        
        histogram3d(binR, binG, binB) = histogram3d(binR, binG, binB)+1; % dodamo na pravo mesto
    end
end

maxValue = max(histogram3d(:));
% vrednost najmocnejsega bina == 255
hist_norm = uint8( (double(histogram3d) / double(maxValue)) * 255 );


hist_presek = VideoWriter("ladja_hist_presek.avi");
hist_presek.FrameRate = ladja.FrameRate;   % enak fps kot original
open(hist_presek);


while hasFrame(ladja)
    frame = double(readFrame(ladja));
    
    [H, W, ~] = size(frame);
    gray = zeros(H, W, 'uint8');
    for c = 1:8:H-7
        for r = 1:8:W-7
            
            hist = zeros(locljivost,locljivost,locljivost); % histogram za 8x8 kvadrat v sliki
            for i = 0:7
                for j = 0:7
                    R = frame(c+i,r+j,1);
                    G = frame(c+i,r+j,2);
                    B = frame(c+i,r+j,3);
                    
                    binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
                    binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
                    binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;

                    hist(binR, binG, binB) = hist(binR, binG, binB) + 1;
                end
            end
            maxValue_obmocje8x8 = max(hist(:));
            % vrednost najmocnejsega bina == 255
            hist_obmocje8x8_norm = uint8( (double(hist) / double(maxValue_obmocje8x8)) * 255 );
      
            % formula za normaliziran presek : sum( min(histogram_morja,  hist8x8) ) / sum(histogram_morja) -> normirani histogrami
            % cez vse bine - manjsa vrednost je presek

            % presek vrednost med 0 in 1
            presek = sum( min(hist_norm(:), hist_obmocje8x8_norm(:)) ) / sum(hist_norm(:));
            val = presek * 256;
            

            gray(c:c+7, r:r+7) = val;  % celo obmocje 8x8 dobi vrednost preseka

        end
    end

    % 3 kanali
    grayRGB = cat(3, gray, gray, gray);

    writeVideo(hist_presek, grayRGB);
end

close(hist_presek);


==== END Domen Hauko | Domen Hauko_21497_assignsubmission_file | vaja3_hauko_turk.m ====

%% RGB

vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(vid, [1 Inf]);

% Dimenzije videa
[H, W, ~, N] = size(frames);

% Izgled 50. framea (Opcionalno)
%frame_50 = frames(:, :, :, 50);

for m = 1:N
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W

            R = frames(i, j, 1, m);
            G = frames(i, j, 2, m);
            B = frames(i, j, 3, m);

            %odredjeno iz koda za min i max vrijednosti
            if (33 <= R && R <= 114 && 63 <= G && G <= 139 && 37 <= B && B <= 129)
                frames(i, j, 1, m) = 255;
                frames(i, j, 2, m) = 255;
                frames(i, j, 3, m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i, j, 1, m) = 0;
                frames(i, j, 2, m) = 0;
                frames(i, j, 3, m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

writer_object = VideoWriter('ladjaRGB.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = 25;
open(writer_object);
writeVideo(writer_object, frames);
close(writer_object);

%% HSV1
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(vid, [1 Inf]);

% dimenzije
[H, W, ~, nFrames] = size(frames);

% konverzija svih frameova u HSV
framesHSV = zeros(H, W, 3, nFrames);
for m = 1:nFrames
    framesHSV(:,:,:,m) = rgb2hsv(frames(:,:,:,m));
end

%min i max za H
Hmin = 0.35;
Hmax = 0.45;

for m = 1:nFrames
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W
            if Hmin <= framesHSV(i,j,1,m) && framesHSV(i,j,1,m) <= Hmax
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

% zapis videa
writer_object = VideoWriter('ladjaHSV1.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = 25;
open(writer_object);
writeVideo(writer_object, frames);
close(writer_object);

%% HSV2

% Učitaj sve frameove videa
ladja  = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(ladja,[1 Inf]);
[H, W, ~, nFrames] = size(frames);

% svi rameovi u HSV
framesHSV = zeros(H, W, 3, nFrames);
for k = 1:nFrames
    framesHSV(:,:,:,k) = rgb2hsv(frames(:,:,:,k));
end

% H i S, min i max
Hmin2 = 0.35;  
Hmax2 = 0.45;
Smin2 = 0.2;  
Smax2 = 0.45;

for m = 1:nFrames
    for i = 1:H
        for j = 1:W
            Hval = framesHSV(i,j,1,m);
            Vval = framesHSV(i,j,3,m);

            if (Hval >= Hmin2 && Hval <= Hmax2 && Vval >= Smin2 && Vval <= Smax2)
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 255;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 255;
            else
                frames(i,j,1,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,2,m) = 0;
                frames(i,j,3,m) = 0;
            end
        end
    end
end

% rezultat
writer_object = VideoWriter('ladjaHSV2.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = 25;
open(writer_object);
writeVideo(writer_object, frames);
close(writer_object);

                frames(i,j,1,m) = 255;

%Jedan frame iz videa
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

[x, y] = ginput(15);

vals = zeros(length(x), 3);

%Očitan RGB za svaku točku
for i = 1:length(x)
    xi = round(x(i));
    yi = round(y(i));
    vals(i, :) = frame(yi, xi, :);
end

%min i max
Rmin = min(vals(:,1));
Rmax = max(vals(:,1));
Gmin = min(vals(:,2));
Gmax = max(vals(:,2));

==== BEGIN Dunja Mihajlović | Dunja Mihajlović_21518_assignsubmission_file | naloga4.m ====
%% NALOGA 4: Avtomatska segmentacija morja z 3D RGB histogramom
clear; close all; clc;

%% Naloži video
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

%%  Preberi prvi frame za učno območje
frame = read(video, 1);
imshow(frame); hold on;
title('Klikni vsaj 3 točke poligona za označitev morja, Enter ko končaš');

%%  Interaktivni poligon za morje
[x, y] = ginput;  % klikni več točk in pritisni Enter

% Preveri, da je vsaj 3 točke
if length(x) < 3
    error('Moraš klikniti vsaj 3 točke za poligon.');
end

% Prikaži poligon
plot([x; x(1)], [y; y(1)], 'r-', 'LineWidth', 2);

%% Ustvarjanje maske z inpolygon (brez poly2mask)
[XX, YY] = meshgrid(1:size(frame,2), 1:size(frame,1));
mask = inpolygon(XX, YY, x, y);

% Prikaz maske
figure;
imshow(mask);
title('Maska izbranega območja');

%%Ekstrakcija pikslov morja
R_sel = double(frame(:,:,1));
G_sel = double(frame(:,:,2));
B_sel = double(frame(:,:,3));

R_sel = R_sel(mask);
G_sel = G_sel(mask);
B_sel = B_sel(mask);

%% Ustvarjanje 3D RGB histograma
numBins = 8;                        % 8x8x8 binov
edges = linspace(0, 255, numBins+1);
hist3D = zeros(numBins, numBins, numBins);

for i = 1:length(R_sel)
    r_bin = find(edges >= R_sel(i),1)-1; r_bin = max(r_bin,1);
    g_bin = find(edges >= G_sel(i),1)-1; g_bin = max(g_bin,1);
    b_bin = find(edges >= B_sel(i),1)-1; b_bin = max(b_bin,1);
    hist3D(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) = hist3D(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) + 1;
end

% Normalizacija histograma (maksimalni bin = 255)
hist3D = uint8(255 * hist3D / max(hist3D(:)));

outVid = VideoWriter('naloga4_output.avi');
outVid.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outVid);

%%  Obdelava vseh frame-ov videa (vektorizirano)
video.CurrentTime = 0;
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);

    Rf = double(frame(:,:,1));
    Gf = double(frame(:,:,2));6
    Bf = double(frame(:,:,3));

    % določitev binov
    r_bin = discretize(Rf, edges);
    g_bin = discretize(Gf, edges);
    b_bin = discretize(Bf, edges);

    % nadomestimo NaN z 1
    r_bin(isnan(r_bin)) = 1;
    g_bin(isnan(g_bin)) = 1;
    b_bin(isnan(b_bin)) = 1;

    % vektorizirana dodelitev sivinskega frame-a
    grayFrame = hist3D(sub2ind(size(hist3D), r_bin, g_bin, b_bin));
    grayFrame = uint8(grayFrame);

    writeVideo(outVid, grayFrame);
end

close(outVid);
disp('Video naloge 4 ustvarjen: naloga4_output.avi');
==== END Dunja Mihajlović | Dunja Mihajlović_21518_assignsubmission_file | naloga4.m ====

==== BEGIN Dunja Mihajlović | Dunja Mihajlović_21518_assignsubmission_file | naloga5.m ====
%% NALOGA 5: Segmentacija z histogram intersection
clear; close all; clc;

%% 1) Naloži video
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video,1);

%% 2) Interaktivni poligon za morje
imshow(frame); hold on;
title('Klikni vsaj 3 točke poligona za morje, Enter ko končaš');
[x, y] = ginput;
if length(x) < 3
    error('Moraš klikniti vsaj 3 točke za poligon.');
end
plot([x;x(1)],[y;y(1)],'r-','LineWidth',2);

[XX, YY] = meshgrid(1:size(frame,2), 1:size(frame,1));
mask = inpolygon(XX, YY, x, y);

%% 3) Histogram morja 4x4x4
numBins = 4;
edges = linspace(0,255,numBins+1);

R_sel = double(frame(:,:,1)); R_sel = R_sel(mask);
G_sel = double(frame(:,:,2)); G_sel = G_sel(mask);
B_sel = double(frame(:,:,3)); B_sel = B_sel(mask);

histSea = zeros(numBins,numBins,numBins);
for i = 1:length(R_sel)
    r_bin = find(edges >= R_sel(i),1)-1; r_bin = max(r_bin,1);
    g_bin = find(edges >= G_sel(i),1)-1; g_bin = max(g_bin,1);
    b_bin = find(edges >= B_sel(i),1)-1; b_bin = max(b_bin,1);
    histSea(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) = histSea(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) + 1;
end
histSea = histSea / sum(histSea(:)); % normaliziramo na vsoto 1

%% 4) Priprava output videa
outVid = VideoWriter('naloga5_output.avi');
outVid.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(outVid);

blockSize = 8;  % 8x8 pikslov

%% 5) Obdelava frame-ov
video.CurrentTime = 0;
while hasFrame(video)
    frame = readFrame(video);
    [H, W, ~] = size(frame);
    
    % Število blokov
    H_blocks = floor(H/blockSize);
    W_blocks = floor(W/blockSize);
    
    grayFrame = zeros(H_blocks*blockSize, W_blocks*blockSize, 'uint8');
    
    % Obdelaj vsak blok
    for i = 1:H_blocks
        for j = 1:W_blocks
            rowIdx = (i-1)*blockSize + (1:blockSize);
            colIdx = (j-1)*blockSize + (1:blockSize);
            
            R_block = double(frame(rowIdx, colIdx,1));
            G_block = double(frame(rowIdx, colIdx,2));
            B_block = double(frame(rowIdx, colIdx,3));
            
            % histogram bloka
            histBlock = zeros(numBins,numBins,numBins);
            for r = 1:blockSize
                for c = 1:blockSize
                    r_bin = find(edges >= R_block(r,c),1)-1; r_bin = max(r_bin,1);
                    g_bin = find(edges >= G_block(r,c),1)-1; g_bin = max(g_bin,1);
                    b_bin = find(edges >= B_block(r,c),1)-1; b_bin = max(b_bin,1);
                    histBlock(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) = histBlock(r_bin,g_bin,b_bin) + 1;
                end
            end
            histBlock = histBlock / sum(histBlock(:));
            
            % Histogram intersection
           intersectVal = sum(min(histSea(:), histBlock(:))) / sum(histSea(:));
            
            % Shrani v sivinski blok (0-256)
            grayBlock = uint8(intersectVal*256);
            grayFrame(rowIdx, colIdx) = grayBlock;
        end
    end
    
    writeVideo(outVid, grayFrame);
end

close(outVid);
disp('Video naloge 5 ustvarjen: naloga5_output.avi');
==== END Dunja Mihajlović | Dunja Mihajlović_21518_assignsubmission_file | naloga5.m ====

y_max = size(zadnji_frame, 1);

vzorec_morja_R = vzorec_morja(:, :, 1);
vzorec_morja_G = vzorec_morja(:, :, 2);
vzorec_morja_B = vzorec_morja(:, :, 3);

R_meje = [min(vzorec_morja_R(:)), max(vzorec_morja_R(:))];
G_meje = [min(vzorec_morja_G(:)), max(vzorec_morja_G(:))];
B_meje = [min(vzorec_morja_B(:)), max(vzorec_morja_B(:))];

video2 = zeros(video.Height, video.Width, video.NumFrames);

for i = 1:video.NumFrames
    for j = 1:video.Height
        for k = 1:video.Width

            if R_meje(1) <= frames(j, k, 1, i) && frames(j, k, 1, i) <= R_meje(2) && ...
               G_meje(1) <= frames(j, k, 2, i) && frames(j, k, 2, i) <= G_meje(2) && ...
               B_meje(1) <= frames(j, k, 3, i) && frames(j, k, 3, i) <= B_meje(2)

                video2(j, k, i) = 255;
            else
                video2(j, k, i) = 0;
            end

        end
    end
end

end
close(writer_RGB);

%% 4 – 3D histogram morja (8x8x8) in preslikava v sivine

video = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frames = read(video, [1 Inf]);
zadnji_frame = frames(:, :, :, video.NumFrames);

x_min = 1;
x_max = size(zadnji_frame, 2);

y_min = size(zadnji_frame, 1) - 179;
y_max = size(zadnji_frame, 1);

vzorec_morja = zadnji_frame(y_min:y_max, x_min:x_max, :);

st_binov = 8;
histogram_vzorca = zeros(st_binov, st_binov, st_binov);

for i = 1:size(vzorec_morja, 1)
    for j = 1:size(vzorec_morja, 2)

        r = floor(double(vzorec_morja(i, j, 1))/(256/st_binov)) + 1;
        g = floor(double(vzorec_morja(i, j, 2))/(256/st_binov)) + 1;
        b = floor(double(vzorec_morja(i, j, 3))/(256/st_binov)) + 1;
        
        histogram_vzorca(r, g, b) = histogram_vzorca(r, g, b) + 1;

    end
end

max_vrednost = max(histogram_vzorca(:));
norm_histogram = histogram_vzorca / max_vrednost * 255;

video2 = zeros(video.Height, video.Width, video.NumFrames);

for i = 1:video.NumFrames
    for j = 1:video.Height
        for k = 1:video.Width

            r = floor(double(frames(j, k, 1, i))/(256/st_binov)) + 1;
            g = floor(double(frames(j, k, 2, i))/(256/st_binov)) + 1;
            b = floor(double(frames(j, k, 3, i))/(256/st_binov)) + 1;

            video2(j, k, i) = norm_histogram(r, g, b);

        end
    end
end

fprintf('Prikaz videa: naloga 4 – 3D histogram barv morja (RGB).\n');
h = implay(video2);
waitfor(h);

writer_4 = VideoWriter('naloga4_histRGB.avi');
open(writer_4);
for i = 1:video.NumFrames
    frame_gray = uint8(video2(:, :, i));
    writeVideo(writer_4, frame_gray);
end
close(writer_4);

%% 5 – histogram intersection po 8x8 blokih (4x4x4, max-bin normalizacija)

video = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frames = read(video, [1 Inf]);
zadnji_frame = frames(:, :, :, video.NumFrames);

x_min = 1;
x_max = size(zadnji_frame, 2);

y_min = size(zadnji_frame, 1) - 179;
y_max = size(zadnji_frame, 1);

vzorec_morja = zadnji_frame(y_min:y_max, x_min:x_max, :);

st_binov = 4;
binSize = 256 / st_binov;

% 5.1 Referenčni histogram morja (RGB, 4x4x4), nato normalizacija z max binom
histogram_vzorca = zeros(st_binov, st_binov, st_binov);

for i = 1:size(vzorec_morja, 1)
    for j = 1:size(vzorec_morja, 2)
        r = floor(double(vzorec_morja(i, j, 1)) / binSize) + 1;
        g = floor(double(vzorec_morja(i, j, 2)) / binSize) + 1;
        b = floor(double(vzorec_morja(i, j, 3)) / binSize) + 1;
        histogram_vzorca(r, g, b) = histogram_vzorca(r, g, b) + 1;
    end
end

% normalizacija modelnega histograma: deljenje z največjo vrednostjo (max bin)
max_model = max(histogram_vzorca(:));
histogram_vzorca_norm = histogram_vzorca / max_model;
vsota_modela_norm = sum(histogram_vzorca_norm(:));

clear vsote

% 5.2 Histogram intersection po 8x8 blokih (Swain & Ballard z max-normalizacijo)
for i = 1:video.NumFrames

    trenutni_frame = frames(:, :, :, i);

    % gremo po blokih 8x8, rob (če ostane) ignoriramo
    for j = 1:8:(video.Height-7)
        for k = 1:8:(video.Width-7)

            podrocje8x8 = trenutni_frame(j:j+7, k:k+7, :);   % 8x8x3, uint8

            Rbin = floor(double(podrocje8x8(:, :, 1)) / binSize) + 1;
            Gbin = floor(double(podrocje8x8(:, :, 2)) / binSize) + 1;
            Bbin = floor(double(podrocje8x8(:, :, 3)) / binSize) + 1;

            idx = sub2ind([st_binov, st_binov, st_binov], Rbin(:), Gbin(:), Bbin(:));

            % histogram bloka (4x4x4) v številu pikslov
            hist_block_vec = accumarray(idx, 1, [st_binov^3, 1]);
            hist_block = reshape(hist_block_vec, [st_binov, st_binov, st_binov]);

            % normalizacija histograma bloka: deljenje z največjo vrednostjo (max bin)
            max_block = max(hist_block(:));
            hist_block_norm = hist_block / max_block;

            % Swain & Ballard histogram intersection:
            % HI = sum_j min(I_j, M_j) / sum_j M_j
            presek = sum(min(hist_block_norm(:), histogram_vzorca_norm(:))) / vsota_modela_norm;

            vsote((j-1)/8+1, (k-1)/8+1, i) = presek;

        end
    end

end

% 5.3 Iz vsot naredimo video: presek (0..1) -> sivina (0..255) – navodila pravijo *256*

video2 = zeros(video.Height, video.Width, video.NumFrames, 'uint8');
visina_blokov  = 8 * size(vsote, 1);
sirina_blokov = 8 * size(vsote, 2);

for i = 1:video.NumFrames

    % presek * 256 za 8-bitno dinamiko (bo saturiralo pri 255)
    blok_sivine = uint8(vsote(:, :, i) * 256);

    % razširimo 8x8 blok v 8x8 pikslov
    video2(1:visina_blokov, 1:sirina_blokov, i) = ...
        kron(blok_sivine, ones(8, 'uint8'));

end

fprintf('Prikaz videa: naloga 5 – histogram intersection po 8x8 blokih.\n');
h = implay(video2);
waitfor(h);

writer_5 = VideoWriter('naloga5_histIntersection.avi');
open(writer_5);
for i = 1:video.NumFrames
    writeVideo(writer_5, video2(:, :, i));   
end
close(writer_5);

fprintf('Program je zaključen.\n');
==== END Filip Kocjančič | Filip Kocjančič_21496_assignsubmission_file | koda_za_oddajo.m ====

%% HSV

%% 3. naloga
C=VideoReader('ladja.avi');
orig_okvirji=read(C,[1 Inf]);
video_size=size(orig_okvirji);
st_frameov=video_size(4);
prvi_frame=orig_okvirji(:,:,:,1);

% pogledaš vrednosti RGB na okvirju
figure; imshow(prvi_frame); impixelinfo;            

Rmin = 0;     Rmax=90;
Gmin = 65;    Gmax=110;
Bmin = 40;    Bmax=120;

Hmin = 0.3;   Hmax=0.5;
Smin = 0;  Smax=0.7;
Vmin = 0;     Vmax=0.6;

rgb_bin_video = VideoWriter('morje_rgb.avi');
rgb_bin_video.FrameRate = 2;
open(rgb_bin_video);

hsv_bin_video = VideoWriter('morje_hsv.avi');
hsv_bin_video.FrameRate = 2;
open(hsv_bin_video);

for i = 1:st_frameov
    R = orig_okvirji(:,:,1,i);
    G = orig_okvirji(:,:,2,i);
    B = orig_okvirji(:,:,3,i);

    maska_rgb = (R>=Rmin & R<=Rmax) & (G>=Gmin & G<=Gmax) & (B>=Bmin & B<=Bmax);
    okvir_rgb = uint8(repmat(maska_rgb, [1 1 3]))*255;     % dobimo 3x matrico
    writeVideo(rgb_bin_video, okvir_rgb);

    I = im2double(orig_okvirji(:,:,:,i));
    okvir_hsv = rgb2hsv(I);
    H = okvir_hsv(:,:,1);   S = okvir_hsv(:,:,2);   V = okvir_hsv(:,:,3);
    maska_hsv = (H>=Hmin & H<=Hmax) & (S>=Smin & S<=Smax) & (V>=Vmin & V<=Vmax);
    okvir_bin_hsv = uint8(repmat(maska_hsv, [1 1 3]))*255;
    writeVideo(hsv_bin_video,okvir_bin_hsv);
end

close(rgb_bin_video);
close(hsv_bin_video);

%% 4. naloga - avtomtska segmentacija
[visina, sirina,~,st_frameov] = size(orig_okvirji);
n_frame=orig_okvirji(:,:,:,20);

% izberi regijo s morjem
figure; imshow(n_frame); title('Izberi regijo z morjem po istem zaporedju kot pri 2. nalogi')
[x2,y2] = ginput(2); close;
x1 =round(min(x2)); y1 = round(min(y2));
x2 =round(max(x2)); y2 = round(max(y2));
regija_interesa = prvi_frame(y1:y2, x1:x2, :);

% narediš tri 1D histograme 0-255               normalizacija
Rh = histcounts(regija_interesa(:,:,1), 0:256); Rh = Rh / max(1, max(Rh));
Gh = histcounts(regija_interesa(:,:,2), 0:256); Gh = Gh / max(1, max(Gh));
Bh = histcounts(regija_interesa(:,:,3), 0:256); Bh = Bh / max(1, max(Bh));

LookUpTabela = @(R,G,B) uint8(255 * ((Rh(R+1) * Gh(G+1) * Bh(B+1))^(1/3)));

auto_hist = VideoWriter('morje_auto_hist.avi');
auto_hist.FrameRate=50;
open(auto_hist);

for i=1:st_frameov
    okvir = orig_okvirji(:,:,:,i);
    R = double(okvir(:,:,1));
    G = double(okvir(:,:,2));
    B = double(okvir(:,:,3));
    
    % vektorsko v sivinski prostor
    Rsiv = double(R)+1;
    Gsiv = double(G)+1;
    Bsiv = double(B)+1;
    
    Y = uint8(255 *  ((Rh(Rsiv).*Gh(Gsiv).*Bh(Bsiv)).^(1/3)));
    
    okvir = repmat(Y,[1 1 3]);
    writeVideo(auto_hist, okvir);
end
close(auto_hist);

%% 5. naloga - segmentacija na podlagi razdalje med histogrami
rob_visina = floor(visina/8)*8; rob_sirina = floor(sirina/8)*8;
n_binov = 4;

vBin = @(C) min(max(floor(double(C)/64) + 1, 1),n_binov);

% 4x4x4 histogram za učenje
Rkvant = vBin(regija_interesa(:,:,1));
Gkvant = vBin(regija_interesa(:,:,2));
Bkvant = vBin(regija_interesa(:,:,3));
bin64 = (Rkvant-1)*16 + (Gkvant-1)*4 + Bkvant;
ref_hist = histcounts(bin64(:), 0.5:1:64.5);
% ref_hist = ref_hist + 1e-9;
% ref_hist= ref_hist / sum(ref_hist);

morje_hist_presek = VideoWriter('morje_hist_presek');
morje_hist_presek.FrameRate = 5;
open(morje_hist_presek);

for i=1:st_frameov
    Rkvant = vBin(orig_okvirji(:,:,1,i));
    Gkvant = vBin(orig_okvirji(:,:,2,i));
    Bkvant = vBin(orig_okvirji(:,:,3,i));

    skupni_bin64 = (Rkvant-1)*16 + (Gkvant-1)*4 + Bkvant;

    Y = zeros(visina,sirina,'uint8');

    % 8x8
    for y=1:8:rob_visina
        for x=1:8:rob_sirina
            
            bin8 = skupni_bin64(y:y+7, x:x+7);

            hist_bin = histcounts(bin8(:), 0.5:1:64.5);
           
            vsota = sum(hist_bin);
            presek  = sum(min(ref_hist, hist_bin));   % Swain Ballord
            presek = presek / vsota;           

            Y(y:y+7, x:x+7) = uint8(max(0, min(255, round(256*presek))));
        end
    end

    okvir = repmat(Y,[1 1 3]);
    writeVideo(morje_hist_presek, okvir);
end
close(morje_hist_presek);

% segmentacija z RGB
ladja = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frame = read(ladja, 5);
%figure;
%imshow(frame);
%impixelinfo;  % doda informacije o pikslih

%52, 79, 51
%95 115, 107

Rmin = 52;  Rmax = 95;
Gmin = 79;  Gmax = 115;
Bmin = 51;  Bmax = 107;

maskRGB = VideoWriter("ladja_mask_RGB.avi");
maskRGB.FrameRate = ladja.FrameRate;   % enak fps kot original
open(maskRGB);


while hasFrame(ladja)
    frame = readFrame(ladja);

    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);

    % piksel je znotraj RGB intervalov - znotraj = 1, zunaj = 0
    mask = (R >= Rmin & R <= Rmax) & ...
           (G >= Gmin & G <= Gmax) & ...
           (B >= Bmin & B <= Bmax);

    % znotraj =  255, zunaj = 0
    gray = uint8(mask) * 255;

    % 3 kanali
    grayRGB = cat(3, gray, gray, gray);

    writeVideo(maskRGB, grayRGB);
end

close(maskRGB);

%% 4. 3D histogram

ladja = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frame = read(ladja, 5);

% morje
% x: 0 -> do konca
% y: 285 -> do konca

morje = frame(320:height(frame), 1:width(frame),:);
%figure;
%imshow(morje);

locljivost = 8;
histogram3d = zeros(locljivost,locljivost,locljivost);

n_na_bin = 256/locljivost;

morje = double(morje);
for col = 1:height(morje)
    for row = 1:width(morje)
        
        R = morje(col,row,1);
        G = morje(col,row,2);
        B = morje(col,row,3);
        
        binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;
        
        histogram3d(binR, binG, binB) = histogram3d(binR, binG, binB)+1; % dodamo na pravo mesto
    end
end

maxValue = max(histogram3d(:));
% vrednost najmocnejsega bina == 255
hist_norm = uint8( (double(histogram3d) / double(maxValue)) * 255 );


hist_segmentacija = VideoWriter("ladja_mask_hist.avi");
hist_segmentacija.FrameRate = ladja.FrameRate;   % enak fps kot original
open(hist_segmentacija);


while hasFrame(ladja)
    frame = double(readFrame(ladja));

    R = frame(:,:,1);
    G = frame(:,:,2);
    B = frame(:,:,3);
    % matrike, kje ima vsak piksel vrednost v kater bin pade v kanalu
    binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
    binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
    binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;

        
    [h, w] = size(R);
    gray = zeros(h, w, 'uint8');
    % piksel v rezultatu (sivinska slika) je vrednost iz histograma 
    for i = 1:h
        for j = 1:w
            gray(i,j) = hist_norm( binR(i,j), binG(i,j), binB(i,j) );
        end
    end

    % 3 kanali
    grayRGB = cat(3, gray, gray, gray);

    writeVideo(hist_segmentacija, grayRGB);
end

close(hist_segmentacija);


%% 5. 3D histogram

ladja = VideoReader("ladja.avi");
frame = read(ladja, 5);

% morje
% x: 0 -> do konca
% y: 285 -> do konca

morje = frame(320:height(frame), 1:width(frame),:);
%figure;
%imshow(morje);

locljivost = 4;
histogram3d = zeros(locljivost,locljivost,locljivost);

n_na_bin = 256/locljivost;

morje = double(morje);
for col = 1:height(morje)
    for row = 1:width(morje)
        
        R = morje(col,row,1);
        G = morje(col,row,2);
        B = morje(col,row,3);
        
        binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
        binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;
        
        histogram3d(binR, binG, binB) = histogram3d(binR, binG, binB)+1; % dodamo na pravo mesto
    end
end

maxValue = max(histogram3d(:));
% vrednost najmocnejsega bina == 255
hist_norm = uint8( (double(histogram3d) / double(maxValue)) * 255 );


hist_presek = VideoWriter("ladja_hist_presek.avi");
hist_presek.FrameRate = ladja.FrameRate;   % enak fps kot original
open(hist_presek);


while hasFrame(ladja)
    frame = double(readFrame(ladja));
    
    [H, W, ~] = size(frame);
    gray = zeros(H, W, 'uint8');
    for c = 1:8:H-7
        for r = 1:8:W-7
            
            hist = zeros(locljivost,locljivost,locljivost); % histogram za 8x8 kvadrat v sliki
            for i = 0:7
                for j = 0:7
                    R = frame(c+i,r+j,1);
                    G = frame(c+i,r+j,2);
                    B = frame(c+i,r+j,3);
                    
                    binR = floor(R / n_na_bin) + 1;
                    binG = floor(G / n_na_bin) + 1;
                    binB = floor(B / n_na_bin) + 1;

                    hist(binR, binG, binB) = hist(binR, binG, binB) + 1;
                end
            end
            maxValue_obmocje8x8 = max(hist(:));
            % vrednost najmocnejsega bina == 255
            hist_obmocje8x8_norm = uint8( (double(hist) / double(maxValue_obmocje8x8)) * 255 );
      
            % formula za normaliziran presek : sum( min(histogram_morja,  hist8x8) ) / sum(histogram_morja) -> normirani histogrami
            % cez vse bine - manjsa vrednost je presek

            % presek vrednost med 0 in 1
            presek = sum( min(hist_norm(:), hist_obmocje8x8_norm(:)) ) / sum(hist_norm(:));
            val = presek * 256;
            

            gray(c:c+7, r:r+7) = val;  % celo obmocje 8x8 dobi vrednost preseka

        end
    end

    % 3 kanali
    grayRGB = cat(3, gray, gray, gray);

    writeVideo(hist_presek, grayRGB);
end

close(hist_presek);

%% 3
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
vid.CurrentTime = 0;
frame = readFrame(vid);
imshow(frame);
disp('klikni dve točki na morju, tako da bosta predstavljali ogljišči pravokotnika');
[x, y] = ginput(2);
close;

min_x = round(min(x));
max_x = round(max(x));
min_y = round(min(y));
max_y = round(max(y));

sea_region = frame(min_x:max_x, min_y:max_y, :);
sea_region_hsv = rgb2hsv(sea_region);

mean_r = mean(sea_region(:,:,1), 'all');
mean_g = mean(sea_region(:,:,2), 'all');
mean_b = mean(sea_region(:,:,3), 'all');
mean_h = mean(sea_region_hsv(:,:,1), 'all');
mean_s = mean(sea_region_hsv(:,:,2), 'all');

tol_rgb = 15;
tol_h = 0.1;
tol_s = 0.1;

%rgb
vid.CurrentTime = 0;
rgb_video = VideoWriter('segmentacija_rgb.avi', 'Uncompressed AVI');
open(rgb_video);

while hasFrame(vid)
    frame_current = readFrame(vid);
    red_mask   = abs(frame_current(:,:,1) - mean_r) <= tol_rgb;
    green_mask = abs(frame_current(:,:,2) - mean_g) <= tol_rgb;
    blue_mask  = abs(frame_current(:,:,3) - mean_b) <= tol_rgb;
    
    combined_mask = red_mask & green_mask & blue_mask;
    binary_frame = uint8(repmat(combined_mask*255, [1,1,3]));
    
    writeVideo(rgb_video, binary_frame);
end

close(rgb_video);

%hsv
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
hsv_video = VideoWriter('segmentacija_hsv.avi', 'Uncompressed AVI');
open(hsv_video);

while hasFrame(vid)
    frame_rgb = readFrame(vid);
    frame_hsv = rgb2hsv(frame_rgb);
    
    hue_mask = abs(frame_hsv(:,:,1) - mean_h) <= tol_h;
    sat_mask = abs(frame_hsv(:,:,2) - mean_s) <= tol_s;
    
    combined_mask = hue_mask & sat_mask;
    binary_frame = uint8(repmat(combined_mask*255, [1,1,3]));
    
    writeVideo(hsv_video, binary_frame);
end

close(hsv_video);


%% 4
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
vid.CurrentTime = 0;
frame = readFrame(vid);
imshow(frame);
disp('klikni dve točki na morju, tako da bosta predstavljali ogljišči pravokotnika');
[x, y] = ginput(2);
close;

x1 = round(min(y)); 
x2 = round(max(y));
y1 = round(min(x)); 
y2 = round(max(x));

sea_sample = frame(x1:x2, y1:y2, :);

num_bins = 8;
bin_edges = linspace(0,1,num_bins+1);

r = double(sea_sample(:,:,1))/255;
g = double(sea_sample(:,:,2))/255;
b = double(sea_sample(:,:,3))/255;

ri = min(floor(r(:)*num_bins)+1, num_bins);
gi = min(floor(g(:)*num_bins)+1, num_bins);
bi = min(floor(b(:)*num_bins)+1, num_bins);

hist3d = zeros(num_bins,num_bins,num_bins);
for k = 1:length(ri)
    hist3d(ri(k), gi(k), bi(k)) = hist3d(ri(k), gi(k), bi(k)) + 1;
end
hist3d = hist3d / max(hist3d(:)) * 255;

seg_video = VideoWriter('ladja_hist_seg.avi','Uncompressed AVI');
open(seg_video);
vid.CurrentTime = 0;

while hasFrame(vid)
    f = double(readFrame(vid))/255;
    ri = min(floor(f(:,:,1)*num_bins)+1, num_bins);
    gi = min(floor(f(:,:,2)*num_bins)+1, num_bins);
    bi = min(floor(f(:,:,3)*num_bins)+1, num_bins);
    
    idx = sub2ind([num_bins,num_bins,num_bins], ri, gi, bi);
    gray_frame = reshape(hist3d(idx), size(f,1), size(f,2));
    
    gray_uint8 = uint8(repmat(gray_frame,[1 1 3]));
    writeVideo(seg_video, gray_uint8);
end

close(seg_video);


%% 5
vid = VideoReader("ladja.avi");

vid.CurrentTime = 0;
F0 = readFrame(vid);
imshow(F0);
disp('klikni dve točki na morju, tako da bosta predstavljali ogljišči pravokotnika');
[x, y] = ginput(2);
close;

r1 = round(min(y)); 
r2 = round(max(y));
c1 = round(min(x)); 
c2 = round(max(x));

roi = F0(r1:r2, c1:c2, :);

bini = 4;
binEdges = linspace(0, 256, bini+1);

binR = discretize(double(roi(:,:,1)), binEdges);
binG = discretize(double(roi(:,:,2)), binEdges);
binB = discretize(double(roi(:,:,3)), binEdges);

refHist = accumarray([binR(:), binG(:), binB(:)], 1, [bini bini bini]);
refHist = refHist / (sum(refHist(:)) + eps);

writer = VideoWriter("ladja_dis_hist.avi");
open(writer);

block = 8;
vid.CurrentTime = 0;

while hasFrame(vid)
    F = readFrame(vid);
    [H, W, ~] = size(F);
    H2 = floor(H/block)*block;
    W2 = floor(W/block)*block;
    F = F(1:H2, 1:W2, :);
    numR = H2/block;
    numC = W2/block;
    inters = zeros(numR, numC);

    for i = 1:numR
        for j = 1:numC
            rA = (i-1)*block + 1;  rB = i*block;
            cA = (j-1)*block + 1;  cB = j*block;
            B = F(rA:rB, cA:cB, :);

            bR = discretize(double(B(:,:,1)), binEdges);
            bG = discretize(double(B(:,:,2)), binEdges);
            bB = discretize(double(B(:,:,3)), binEdges);

            Hblk = accumarray([bR(:), bG(:), bB(:)], 1, [bini bini bini]);
            Hblk = Hblk / (sum(Hblk(:)) + eps);

            inters(i,j) = sum(min(Hblk(:), refHist(:)));
        end
    end

    outFrame = kron(inters, ones(block));
    outFrame = uint8(outFrame * 256);
    outFrame = repmat(outFrame, 1,1,3);
    writeVideo(writer, outFrame);
end

close(writer);

S1 = uint8(255*p_hsv(:,:,2));
V1 = uint8(255*p_hsv(:,:,3));

figure;

subplot(2,2,2); imshow(H1); title('H');
subplot(2,2,3); imshow(S1); title('S');
subplot(2,2,4); imshow(V1); title('V');

y_hsv = rgb2hsv(yosemite);
H2 = uint8(255*y_hsv(:,:,1));
S2 = uint8(255*y_hsv(:,:,2));
V2 = uint8(255*y_hsv(:,:,3));

subplot(2,2,3); imshow(S2); title('S');

b1 = uint8(255*mat2gray(p_lab(:,:,3)));
