%% 3
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
vid.CurrentTime = 0;
frame = readFrame(vid);
imshow(frame);
disp('klikni dve točki na morju, tako da bosta predstavljali ogljišči pravokotnika');
[x, y] = ginput(2);
close;

min_x = round(min(x));
max_x = round(max(x));
min_y = round(min(y));
max_y = round(max(y));

sea_region = frame(min_x:max_x, min_y:max_y, :);
sea_region_hsv = rgb2hsv(sea_region);

mean_r = mean(sea_region(:,:,1), 'all');
mean_g = mean(sea_region(:,:,2), 'all');
mean_b = mean(sea_region(:,:,3), 'all');
mean_h = mean(sea_region_hsv(:,:,1), 'all');
mean_s = mean(sea_region_hsv(:,:,2), 'all');

tol_rgb = 15;
tol_h = 0.1;
tol_s = 0.1;

%rgb
vid.CurrentTime = 0;
rgb_video = VideoWriter('segmentacija_rgb.avi', 'Uncompressed AVI');
open(rgb_video);

while hasFrame(vid)
    frame_current = readFrame(vid);
    red_mask   = abs(frame_current(:,:,1) - mean_r) <= tol_rgb;
    green_mask = abs(frame_current(:,:,2) - mean_g) <= tol_rgb;
    blue_mask  = abs(frame_current(:,:,3) - mean_b) <= tol_rgb;
    
    combined_mask = red_mask & green_mask & blue_mask;
    binary_frame = uint8(repmat(combined_mask*255, [1,1,3]));
    
    writeVideo(rgb_video, binary_frame);
end

close(rgb_video);

%hsv
vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
hsv_video = VideoWriter('segmentacija_hsv.avi', 'Uncompressed AVI');
open(hsv_video);

while hasFrame(vid)
    frame_rgb = readFrame(vid);
    frame_hsv = rgb2hsv(frame_rgb);
    
    hue_mask = abs(frame_hsv(:,:,1) - mean_h) <= tol_h;
    sat_mask = abs(frame_hsv(:,:,2) - mean_s) <= tol_s;
    
    combined_mask = hue_mask & sat_mask;
    binary_frame = uint8(repmat(combined_mask*255, [1,1,3]));
    
    writeVideo(hsv_video, binary_frame);
end

close(hsv_video);

% Naloga 1 Koncept barv in barvni prostori RGB, HSV ter Lab
% Jakob Suhadolc & Mihaela Temelkova

slike = { 'yosemite_meadows.jpg', ...
          'coloured_pencils.jpg' };

%PRETVORBA V HSV PROSTORU
for i = 1:numel(slike)
    
    % Preberi RGB sliko
    I = imread(slike{i});
    I_d = im2double(I); %vrednosti pikslov preslika iz [0,255] v [0,1]

    %Pretvori RGB sliko v HSV in dobimo tri kanale slike
    I_hsv = rgb2hsv(I_d);
    H = I_hsv(:,:,1);
    S = I_hsv(:,:,2);
    V = I_hsv(:,:,3);

    figure('Name',['HSV - ' slike{i}]);

    subplot(2,2,1);
    imshow(I);
    title(['Original: ' slike{i}], 'Interpreter','none');

    subplot(2,2,2);
    imagesc(H);
    axis image off;
    title('H komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,3);
    imagesc(S);
    axis image off;
    title('S komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,4);
    imagesc(V);
    axis image off;
    title('V komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

% PRETVORBA V Lab PROSTORU
    I_lab = rgb2lab(I_d);
    L = I_lab(:,:,1);   
    a = I_lab(:,:,2);   
    b = I_lab(:,:,3);  

    figure('Name',['Lab - ' slike{i}]);

    subplot(2,2,1);
    imshow(I);
    title(['Original: ' slike{i}], 'Interpreter','none');

    subplot(2,2,2);
    imagesc(L, [0 100]);
    axis image off;
    title('L komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,3);
    imagesc(a, [-128 127]);
    axis image off;
    title('a komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,4);
    imagesc(b, [-128 127]);
    axis image off;
    title('b komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

end

%% Naloga 2 Vpliv posameznih komponent na rekonstruirano RGB sliko
close all; clear; clc;

slike = {'yosemite_meadows.jpg', 'coloured_pencils.jpg' };

% Izkloplenje HSV komponent
for i = 1:numel(slike)

    I = im2double(imread(slike{i}));
    HSV = rgb2hsv(I);
    % Ena komponenta je konstanta, z namenom da jo izklopimo in vidimo
    % njeneno delovanje 
    H = HSV;
    H(:,:,1) = 0.5;
    S = HSV;
    S(:,:,2) = 0.5;
    V = HSV;
    V(:,:,3) = 0.5;

    I_H = hsv2rgb(H);
    I_S = hsv2rgb(S);
    I_V = hsv2rgb(V);

    figure('Name', slike{i});
    subplot(2,2,1); imshow(I); title('Original');
    subplot(2,2,2); imshow(I_H); title('H = 0.5');
    subplot(2,2,3); imshow(I_S); title('S = 0.5');
    subplot(2,2,4); imshow(I_V); title('V = 0.5');
end

%% Naloga 2
close all; clear; clc;

slike = {'yosemite_meadows.jpg', 'coloured_pencils.jpg' };

%Izkloplenje Lab komponent

for i = 1:numel(slike)
    I = im2double(imread(slike{i}));
    Lab = rgb2lab(I);

    L = Lab;
    L(:,:,1) = 50; % Od 0 do 100
    a = Lab;
    a(:,:,2) = 0;  %Od -127 do 128
    b = Lab;
    b(:,:,3) = 0; %Od -127 do 128

    I_L = lab2rgb(L);
    I_a = lab2rgb(a);
    I_b = lab2rgb(b);

    figure('Name', slike{i});
    subplot(2,2,1); imshow(I); title('Original');
    subplot(2,2,2); imshow(I_L); title('L = 50');
    subplot(2,2,3); imshow(I_a); title('a = 0');
    subplot(2,2,4); imshow(I_b); title('b = 0');
end

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_21500_assignsubmission_file | Naloga3_Vaja3.m ====
%% Naloga 3 – segmentacija morja v RGB in HSV
close all; clear; clc;

% Preberemo prvi frame iz videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = readFrame(v);
framed = im2double(frame); 

% Uporabnik klikne točko na morju (za določitev patcha)
figure; imshow(framed); title('Klikni na morje');
[x, y] = ginput(1);
x = round(x); y = round(y);
close;

% Izrežemo manjšo zaplato (patch) okoli klika
r = 50;
[H_img, W_img, ~] = size(framed);
x1 = max(1, x-r); x2 = min(W_img, x+r);
y1 = max(1, y-r); y2 = min(H_img, y+r);

%% RGB segmentacija 

% Patch nad morjem v RGB, ki ga naredimo iz okolice tocke ki smo jo
% kliknili
patchRGB = framed(y1:y2, x1:x2, :);

% Povprečne RGB vrednosti patcha (tipična barva morja)
mRGB = squeeze(mean(mean(patchRGB,1),2));

% Dovoljen odmik okoli povprečij 
dR = 0.10; dG = 0.10; dB = 0.10;

% Intervali za R, G, B in definicija barve morja
Rmin = max(0, mRGB(1)-dR); Rmax = min(1, mRGB(1)+dR);
Gmin = max(0, mRGB(2)-dG); Gmax = min(1, mRGB(2)+dG);
Bmin = max(0, mRGB(3)-dB); Bmax = min(1, mRGB(3)+dB);

% Ponovno odpremo video in pripravimo izhodni RGB video
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
outRGB = VideoWriter('ladja_morje_RGB.avi');
outRGB.FrameRate = v.FrameRate;
open(outRGB);

% Za vsak frame preverimo, kateri piksli so znotraj RGB intervalov
while hasFrame(v)
    frm = im2double(readFrame(v));
    R = frm(:,:,1); G = frm(:,:,2); B = frm(:,:,3);

    % Maska: 1 - barva podobna morju, 0 - ni 
    mask = (R>=Rmin & R<=Rmax) & ...
           (G>=Gmin & G<=Gmax) & ...
           (B>=Bmin & B<=Bmax);

    % Pretvorba maske v binarno (0/255) in v 3-kanalno sliko za video
    BW = uint8(mask)*255;
    BW_rgb = cat(3,BW,BW,BW);
    writeVideo(outRGB, BW_rgb);
end

close(outRGB);

%%  HSV segmentacija

% Patch nad morjem pretvorimo v HSV
patchHSV = rgb2hsv(patchRGB);

% Povprečne HSV vrednosti patcha
mHSV = squeeze(mean(mean(patchHSV,1),2));

% Dovoljen odmik za H in S (V ignoriramo)
dH = 0.10; dS = 0.25;        

% Intervali za H in S in definicija barve morja v HSV
Hmin = max(0, mHSV(1)-dH); Hmax = min(1, mHSV(1)+dH);
Smin = max(0, mHSV(2)-dS); Smax = min(1, mHSV(2)+dS);

% Ponovno odpremo video in pripravimo izhodni binarni video (HSV prag)
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
outHSV = VideoWriter('ladja_morje_HSV.avi');
outHSV.FrameRate = v.FrameRate;
open(outHSV);

% Za vsak frame naredimo vpragovljenje v HSV prostoru
while hasFrame(v)
    frm = im2double(readFrame(v));
    hsv = rgb2hsv(frm);
    Hc = hsv(:,:,1); Sc = hsv(:,:,2);

    % Maska: 1 - piksel ima H,S podobna morju
    mask = (Hc>=Hmin & Hc<=Hmax) & ...
           (Sc>=Smin & Sc<=Smax);  

    % Pretvorimo masko v binarno sliko in zapišemo v video
    BW = uint8(mask)*255;
    BW_rgb = cat(3,BW,BW,BW);
    writeVideo(outHSV, BW_rgb);
end

close(outHSV);
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_21500_assignsubmission_file | Naloga3_Vaja3.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_21500_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4_Vaja3.m ====
%% Naloga 4
close all; clear; clc;

% Preberemo prvi frame videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = readFrame(v);
framed = im2double(frame);

% Kliknemo točko na morju
figure; imshow(framed); title('Klikni na morje');
[x, y] = ginput(1);
x = round(x); y = round(y);
close;

% Izrežemo patch okoli klika (morje)
r = 50;
[H_img, W_img, ~] = size(framed);
x1 = max(1, x-r); x2 = min(W_img, x+r);
y1 = max(1, y-r); y2 = min(H_img, y+r);

% učenje barv morja
patch = framed(y1:y2, x1:x2, :);  

% Razdelimo vsak kanal R,G,B na 8 delov ali 8x8x8
bins = 8;
%Definiramo robove bina oziroma bin 1 je na intervalu [0,0.125], bin 2 na [0.125,0.25]
edges = linspace(0,1,bins+1);
% Dolocimo 8x8x8 matrika H oziroma histograma ki ima 0
H = zeros(bins,bins,bins);

% 3D RGB histogram patcha
R = patch(:,:,1); G = patch(:,:,2); B = patch(:,:,3);
% Pove v katerem binu se nahaja piksel iz patcha glede na edges
ri = discretize(R,edges);
gi = discretize(G,edges);
bi = discretize(B,edges);

%Steje piksle, koliko pikslov se pojavijo v posamezen bin
sz = size(patch,1)*size(patch,2);
for i=1:sz
    H(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) = H(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) + 1;
end

% Normalizacija histograma na [0,255]
H = H / max(H(:));
H = H * 255;

% Gremo čez cel video in vsako točko preslikamo skozi histogram
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
writer = VideoWriter('ladja_Naloga4.avi');  
open(writer);

while hasFrame(v)
    f = im2double(readFrame(v));
    R = f(:,:,1); G = f(:,:,2); B = f(:,:,3);

    ri = discretize(R,edges);
    gi = discretize(G,edges);
    bi = discretize(B,edges);

    M = zeros(size(R));          % izhodna sivinska slika
    for i=1:numel(R)
        M(i) = H(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i));   % vrednost iz histograma
    end

    M = uint8(M);
    writeVideo(writer, M);       % zapišemo frame
end

close(writer);
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_21500_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4_Vaja3.m ====

==== BEGIN Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_21500_assignsubmission_file | Naloga5_Vaja3.m ====
%% Naloga 5 - Segmentacija z uporabo histogram intersection

close all; clear; clc;

% Preberemo prvi frame iz videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = readFrame(v);
framed = im2double(frame);

%Izbere pravokotnik nad morjem (nas patch)
figure; imshow(framed);
title('Selektiraj pravokotnik cez morja');
patch_rect = round(getrect); % [x, y, width, height]

% Koordinate pravokotnika
px = patch_rect(1);  
py = patch_rect(2);
pw = patch_rect(3);  
ph = patch_rect(4);

% Izrežemo patch morja iz slike
patch = framed(py:py+ph, px:px+pw, :);

% Razdelimo R,G,B kanale na 4 delov, kar bo nas referencni histogram
bins = 4;                                   
edges = linspace(0,1,bins+1); 
% prazen 3D histogram 4x4x4
H_ref = zeros(bins,bins,bins);           

% Kanali patcha (morje)
R = patch(:,:,1); G = patch(:,:,2); B = patch(:,:,3);

% Za vsak piksel določimo, v kateri bin
ri = discretize(R, edges);
gi = discretize(G, edges);
bi = discretize(B, edges);

% Polnimo 3D histogram: štejemo, kolikokrat se pojavi kombinacija (ri,gi,bi)
for i = 1:numel(R)
    H_ref(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) = H_ref(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) + 1;
end

% Normaliziramo referenčni histogram
H_ref = H_ref / sum(H_ref(:));

% Priprava videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
writer = VideoWriter('ladja_morje_Naloga5.avi');
open(writer);

block = 8;   % velikost bloka: 8×8 pikslov

% Gremo čez vse frame-e videa
while hasFrame(v)
    frame = im2double(readFrame(v));
    [h, w, ~] = size(frame);

    % izhodna sivinska mapa podobnosti z morjem
    M = zeros(h, w);  

    % Premikamo se po sliki v korakih 8 px po y in x
    for y = 1:block:(h - mod(h,block))
        for x = 1:block:(w - mod(w,block))

            % Izrežemo trenutni blok velikosti 8x8
            blk = frame(y:y+block-1, x:x+block-1, :);

            %Izračun histograma za ta blok (Hb) 
            Hb = zeros(bins,bins,bins);    

            Rb = blk(:,:,1);
            Gb = blk(:,:,2);
            Bb = blk(:,:,3);

            % Določimo indekse binov za kanal R,G,B v bloku
            rbi = discretize(Rb, edges);
            gbi = discretize(Gb, edges);
            bbi = discretize(Bb, edges);

            % Polnimo histogram bloka: štejemo pojavitve kombinacij (rbi,gbi,bbi)
            for i = 1:numel(Rb)
                Hb(rbi(i), gbi(i), bbi(i)) = Hb(rbi(i), gbi(i), bbi(i)) + 1;
            end

            % Normaliziramo histogram bloka, če ni prazen (vsota = 1)
            if sum(Hb(:)) > 0
                Hb = Hb / sum(Hb(:));
            end

            % Histogram intersection (Swain & Ballard)
             % HI meri podobnost med histogramom morja (H_ref) in histogramom trenutnega bloka (Hb)
            HI = sum( min(H_ref(:), Hb(:)) );

            % Preslikamo HI v interval 0–255 za sivinsko sliko
            HI_val = uint8(HI * 256);

            % Celoten blok dobi isto sivinsko vrednost (HI_val)
            % Svetel blok - barve podobne morju, temen blok - drugačne barve
            M(y:y+block-1, x:x+block-1) = HI_val;
        end
    end

    % Zapišemo sivinski frame v video
    writeVideo(writer, uint8(M));
end

close(writer);

disp('konec');
==== END Jakob Suhadolc | Jakob Suhadolc_21500_assignsubmission_file | Naloga5_Vaja3.m ====

vidObj = VideoReader('ladja.avi');  
nFrames = vidObj.NumFrames;         
frameExample = read(vidObj,1);      
imshow(frameExample);               
title('Select a ROI that contains the sea');

h = imrect;             
position = round(wait(h)); 
x1 = position(1); y1 = position(2);
w = position(3); hgt = position(4);
roi = frameExample(y1:y1+hgt, x1:x1+w, :);  

roi = im2double(roi);  
R = roi(:,:,1);
G = roi(:,:,2);
B = roi(:,:,3);

Rmin = min(R(:)); Rmax = max(R(:));
Gmin = min(G(:)); Gmax = max(G(:));
Bmin = min(B(:)); Bmax = max(B(:));

fprintf('R range: %.2f - %.2f\n', Rmin, Rmax);
fprintf('G range: %.2f - %.2f\n', Gmin, Gmax);
fprintf('B range: %.2f - %.2f\n', Bmin, Bmax);


outputRGB = VideoWriter('morje_RGB.avi'); 
outputRGB.FrameRate = vidObj.FrameRate;
open(outputRGB);

for k = 1:nFrames
    frame = im2double(read(vidObj,k));
    
    mask = frame(:,:,1) >= Rmin & frame(:,:,1) <= Rmax & ...
           frame(:,:,2) >= Gmin & frame(:,:,2) <= Gmax & ...
           frame(:,:,3) >= Bmin & frame(:,:,3) <= Bmax;
    
    maskRGB = repmat(uint8(mask*255), [1 1 3]);
    
    writeVideo(outputRGB, maskRGB);
end
close(outputRGB);
disp('RGB segmentation finished!');

    frame = im2double(read(vidObj,k));

           hsvFrame(:,:,2) >= Smin & hsvFrame(:,:,2) <= Smax;

close all; clc;

vidObj = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame1 = im2double(read(vidObj,1));
imshow(frame1); 
title('Izberi ROI za morje');

h = drawrectangle('Color','r');  

disp('Ko končaš z izbiro, pritisni Enter v Command Window');
pause;  

position = round(h.Position);

             position(1):position(1)+position(3), :);

figure; imshow(roi); title('Izbrano območje morja');


nBins = 8; 

R_roi = roi(:,:,1); G_roi = roi(:,:,2); B_roi = roi(:,:,3);

edges = linspace(0,1,nBins+1);

hist3D = zeros(nBins,nBins,nBins);

for i = 1:numel(R_roi)
    rBin = find(R_roi(i) >= edges,1,'last'); rBin = min(rBin,nBins);
    gBin = find(G_roi(i) >= edges,1,'last'); gBin = min(gBin,nBins);
    bBin = find(B_roi(i) >= edges,1,'last'); bBin = min(bBin,nBins);
    hist3D(rBin,gBin,bBin) = hist3D(rBin,gBin,bBin) + 1;
end

hist3D = hist3D / max(hist3D(:)) * 255;

outputHist = VideoWriter('morje_histogram.avi');
open(outputHist);

for k = 1:nFrames
    frame = im2double(read(vidObj,k));
    [rows, cols, ~] = size(frame);
    grayFrame = zeros(rows,cols);
    
    Rf = frame(:,:,1); Gf = frame(:,:,2); Bf = frame(:,:,3);
    
    for i = 1:rows
        for j = 1:cols
            rBin = find(Rf(i,j) >= edges,1,'last'); rBin = min(rBin,nBins);
            gBin = find(Gf(i,j) >= edges,1,'last'); gBin = min(gBin,nBins);
            bBin = find(Bf(i,j) >= edges,1,'last'); bBin = min(bBin,nBins);
            
            grayFrame(i,j) = hist3D(rBin,gBin,bBin); 
        end
    end
    
    grayFrame_uint8 = uint8(grayFrame);
    
    writeVideo(outputHist, grayFrame_uint8);
end

close(outputHist);
disp('Histogram segmentacija končana in shranjena kot morje_histogram.avi');

==== BEGIN Lina Josifovska | Lina Josifovska_21506_assignsubmission_file | naloga5.m ====
close all; clc;

vidObj = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
nFrames = vidObj.NumFrames;

frame1 = im2double(read(vidObj,1));
imshow(frame1); title('Izberi ROI za morje'); 
h = drawrectangle('Color','r'); 
disp('Ko končaš z izbiro, pritisni Enter v Command Window'); pause;
position = round(h.Position);
roi = frame1(position(2):position(2)+position(4), position(1):position(1)+position(3), :);
delete(h);

nBins = 4;
edges = linspace(0,1,nBins+1);
R_roi = roi(:,:,1); G_roi = roi(:,:,2); B_roi = roi(:,:,3);
histROI = zeros(nBins,nBins,nBins);

for i = 1:numel(R_roi)
    rBin = find(R_roi(i) >= edges,1,'last'); rBin = min(rBin,nBins);
    gBin = find(G_roi(i) >= edges,1,'last'); gBin = min(gBin,nBins);
    bBin = find(B_roi(i) >= edges,1,'last'); bBin = min(bBin,nBins);
    histROI(rBin,gBin,bBin) = histROI(rBin,gBin,bBin) + 1;
end

histROI = histROI / sum(histROI(:)); 

blockSize = 8; 
outputHist = VideoWriter('morje_hist_intersection.avi');
open(outputHist);

for k = 1:nFrames
    frame = im2double(read(vidObj,k));
    [rows, cols, ~] = size(frame);
    
    grayFrame = zeros(rows,cols);
    
    for i = 1:blockSize:rows-blockSize+1
        for j = 1:blockSize:cols-blockSize+1
            block = frame(i:i+blockSize-1, j:j+blockSize-1, :);
            
            Rb = block(:,:,1); Gb = block(:,:,2); Bb = block(:,:,3);
            histBlock = zeros(nBins,nBins,nBins);
            
            for p = 1:numel(Rb)
                rBin = find(Rb(p) >= edges,1,'last'); rBin = min(rBin,nBins);
                gBin = find(Gb(p) >= edges,1,'last'); gBin = min(gBin,nBins);
                bBin = find(Bb(p) >= edges,1,'last'); bBin = min(bBin,nBins);
                histBlock(rBin,gBin,bBin) = histBlock(rBin,gBin,bBin) + 1;
            end
            
            histBlock = histBlock / sum(histBlock(:)); 
            
            intersection = sum(min(histROI(:), histBlock(:))); 
            intensity = uint8(intersection * 256); 
            
            grayFrame(i:i+blockSize-1, j:j+blockSize-1) = intensity;
        end
    end
    
    writeVideo(outputHist, uint8(grayFrame));
end

close(outputHist);
disp('Segmentacija po histogram intersection končana, shranjeno kot morje_hist_intersection.avi');

G_idx = floor(sea_section(:,:,2) / step) + 1;
B_idx = floor(sea_section(:,:,3) / step) + 1;

rgb_bins = accumarray([R_idx(:), G_idx(:), B_idx(:)], 1, [num_bins num_bins num_bins]);

hist_section = round(rgb_bins / (max(rgb_bins(:))/255));

    G_idx = floor(video(:,:,2,n) / step) + 1;
    B_idx = floor(video(:,:,3,n) / step) + 1;

    for i = 1:size(video, 1)
        for j = 1:size(video, 2)
            video_gray(i, j, 1, n) = hist_section(R_idx(i,j), G_idx(i,j), B_idx(i,j));

close all;

video_obj = VideoReader('01-Input\ladja.avi');
video = read(video_obj);
sea_section = video(300:449, 1:399,:,30);
f1 = image(sea_section);
video = double(video);
sea_section = double(sea_section);

num_bins = 4;
step = 256/num_bins;

R_idx = floor(sea_section(:,:,1) / step) + 1;
G_idx = floor(sea_section(:,:,2) / step) + 1;
B_idx = floor(sea_section(:,:,3) / step) + 1;

rgb_bins = accumarray([R_idx(:), G_idx(:), B_idx(:)], 1, [num_bins num_bins num_bins]);

hist_section = rgb_bins / sum(rgb_bins(:));

f2 = figure;
[x,y,z] = ndgrid(1:num_bins,1:num_bins,1:num_bins);
scatter3(x(:), y(:), z(:), 100, hist_section(:), 'filled');
xlabel('R'); ylabel('G'); zlabel('B');
colormap(sky);
colorbar;
axis equal;



video_razlika_hist = zeros(size(video, 1), size(video, 2), 1, size(video, 4));


for n = 1:size(video, 4)
    section_count = 0;  
    section_x = 8;
    while section_x <= size(video, 1)
        section_y = 8;
        while section_y <= size(video, 2)
            section8 = video((section_x - 7):section_x,(section_y - 7):section_y,:,n);
            section_count = section_count + 1;     
            
            R_idx = floor(section8(:,:,1) / step) + 1;
            G_idx = floor(section8(:,:,2) / step) + 1;
            B_idx = floor(section8(:,:,3) / step) + 1;
    
            rgb_bins = accumarray([R_idx(:), G_idx(:), B_idx(:)], 1, [num_bins num_bins num_bins]);
            rgb_bins = rgb_bins / sum(rgb_bins(:));
            presek = hist_presek(rgb_bins, hist_section);
    
            video_razlika_hist((section_x - 7):section_x, (section_y - 7):section_y, 1, n) = presek;

            section_y = section_y + 8;
            
        end
        section_x = section_x + 8;
    end
end

video_razlika_hist = repmat(video_razlika_hist, [1, 1, 3, 1]);

writer_object = VideoWriter('02-Output\video_razlika_histogram.avi');
writer_object.FrameRate = video_obj.FrameRate;
writer_object.Quality = 100;
open(writer_object)
writeVideo(writer_object, uint8(video_razlika_hist))
close(writer_object)

function presek = hist_presek(hist, ref_hist)
    presek = sum(min(hist(:), ref_hist(:)));
    presek = presek * 255;
end

% Naloga 1 Koncept barv in barvni prostori RGB, HSV ter Lab
% Jakob Suhadolc & Mihaela Temelkova

slike = { 'yosemite_meadows.jpg', ...
          'coloured_pencils.jpg' };

%PRETVORBA V HSV PROSTORU
for i = 1:numel(slike)
    
    % Preberi RGB sliko
    I = imread(slike{i});
    I_d = im2double(I); %vrednosti pikslov preslika iz [0,255] v [0,1]

    %Pretvori RGB sliko v HSV in dobimo tri kanale slike
    I_hsv = rgb2hsv(I_d);
    H = I_hsv(:,:,1);
    S = I_hsv(:,:,2);
    V = I_hsv(:,:,3);

    figure('Name',['HSV - ' slike{i}]);

    subplot(2,2,1);
    imshow(I);
    title(['Original: ' slike{i}], 'Interpreter','none');

    subplot(2,2,2);
    imagesc(H);
    axis image off;
    title('H komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,3);
    imagesc(S);
    axis image off;
    title('S komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,4);
    imagesc(V);
    axis image off;
    title('V komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

% PRETVORBA V Lab PROSTORU
    I_lab = rgb2lab(I_d);
    L = I_lab(:,:,1);   
    a = I_lab(:,:,2);   
    b = I_lab(:,:,3);  

    figure('Name',['Lab - ' slike{i}]);

    subplot(2,2,1);
    imshow(I);
    title(['Original: ' slike{i}], 'Interpreter','none');

    subplot(2,2,2);
    imagesc(L, [0 100]);
    axis image off;
    title('L komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,3);
    imagesc(a, [-128 127]);
    axis image off;
    title('a komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

    subplot(2,2,4);
    imagesc(b, [-128 127]);
    axis image off;
    title('b komponenta');
    colormap(gray); colorbar;

end

%% Naloga 2 Vpliv posameznih komponent na rekonstruirano RGB sliko
close all; clear; clc;

slike = {'yosemite_meadows.jpg', 'coloured_pencils.jpg' };

% Izkloplenje HSV komponent
for i = 1:numel(slike)

    I = im2double(imread(slike{i}));
    HSV = rgb2hsv(I);
    % Ena komponenta je konstanta, z namenom da jo izklopimo in vidimo
    % njeneno delovanje 
    H = HSV;
    H(:,:,1) = 0.5;
    S = HSV;
    S(:,:,2) = 0.5;
    V = HSV;
    V(:,:,3) = 0.5;

    I_H = hsv2rgb(H);
    I_S = hsv2rgb(S);
    I_V = hsv2rgb(V);

    figure('Name', slike{i});
    subplot(2,2,1); imshow(I); title('Original');
    subplot(2,2,2); imshow(I_H); title('H = 0.5');
    subplot(2,2,3); imshow(I_S); title('S = 0.5');
    subplot(2,2,4); imshow(I_V); title('V = 0.5');
end

    b = Lab;
    b(:,:,3) = 0; %Od -127 do 128

    I_L = lab2rgb(L);
    I_a = lab2rgb(a);
    I_b = lab2rgb(b);

    figure('Name', slike{i});
    subplot(2,2,1); imshow(I); title('Original');

end

==== BEGIN Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_21505_assignsubmission_file | Naloga3_Vaja3.m ====
%% Naloga 3 – segmentacija morja v RGB in HSV
close all; clear; clc;

% Preberemo prvi frame iz videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = readFrame(v);
framed = im2double(frame); 

% Uporabnik klikne točko na morju (za določitev patcha)
figure; imshow(framed); title('Klikni na morje');
[x, y] = ginput(1);
x = round(x); y = round(y);
close;

% Izrežemo manjšo zaplato (patch) okoli klika
r = 50;
[H_img, W_img, ~] = size(framed);
x1 = max(1, x-r); x2 = min(W_img, x+r);
y1 = max(1, y-r); y2 = min(H_img, y+r);

%% RGB segmentacija 

% Patch nad morjem v RGB, ki ga naredimo iz okolice tocke ki smo jo
% kliknili
patchRGB = framed(y1:y2, x1:x2, :);

% Povprečne RGB vrednosti patcha (tipična barva morja)
mRGB = squeeze(mean(mean(patchRGB,1),2));

% Dovoljen odmik okoli povprečij 
dR = 0.10; dG = 0.10; dB = 0.10;

% Intervali za R, G, B in definicija barve morja
Rmin = max(0, mRGB(1)-dR); Rmax = min(1, mRGB(1)+dR);
Gmin = max(0, mRGB(2)-dG); Gmax = min(1, mRGB(2)+dG);
Bmin = max(0, mRGB(3)-dB); Bmax = min(1, mRGB(3)+dB);

% Ponovno odpremo video in pripravimo izhodni RGB video
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
outRGB = VideoWriter('ladja_morje_RGB.avi');
outRGB.FrameRate = v.FrameRate;
open(outRGB);

% Za vsak frame preverimo, kateri piksli so znotraj RGB intervalov
while hasFrame(v)
    frm = im2double(readFrame(v));
    R = frm(:,:,1); G = frm(:,:,2); B = frm(:,:,3);

    % Maska: 1 - barva podobna morju, 0 - ni 
    mask = (R>=Rmin & R<=Rmax) & ...
           (G>=Gmin & G<=Gmax) & ...
           (B>=Bmin & B<=Bmax);

    % Pretvorba maske v binarno (0/255) in v 3-kanalno sliko za video
    BW = uint8(mask)*255;
    BW_rgb = cat(3,BW,BW,BW);
    writeVideo(outRGB, BW_rgb);
end

close(outRGB);

%%  HSV segmentacija

% Patch nad morjem pretvorimo v HSV
patchHSV = rgb2hsv(patchRGB);

% Povprečne HSV vrednosti patcha
mHSV = squeeze(mean(mean(patchHSV,1),2));

% Dovoljen odmik za H in S (V ignoriramo)
dH = 0.10; dS = 0.25;        

% Intervali za H in S in definicija barve morja v HSV
Hmin = max(0, mHSV(1)-dH); Hmax = min(1, mHSV(1)+dH);
Smin = max(0, mHSV(2)-dS); Smax = min(1, mHSV(2)+dS);

% Ponovno odpremo video in pripravimo izhodni binarni video (HSV prag)
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
outHSV = VideoWriter('ladja_morje_HSV.avi');
outHSV.FrameRate = v.FrameRate;
open(outHSV);

% Za vsak frame naredimo vpragovljenje v HSV prostoru
while hasFrame(v)
    frm = im2double(readFrame(v));
    hsv = rgb2hsv(frm);
    Hc = hsv(:,:,1); Sc = hsv(:,:,2);

    % Maska: 1 - piksel ima H,S podobna morju
    mask = (Hc>=Hmin & Hc<=Hmax) & ...
           (Sc>=Smin & Sc<=Smax);  

    % Pretvorimo masko v binarno sliko in zapišemo v video
    BW = uint8(mask)*255;
    BW_rgb = cat(3,BW,BW,BW);
    writeVideo(outHSV, BW_rgb);
end

close(outHSV);
==== END Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_21505_assignsubmission_file | Naloga3_Vaja3.m ====

==== BEGIN Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_21505_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4_Vaja3.m ====
%% Naloga 4
close all; clear; clc;

% Preberemo prvi frame videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = readFrame(v);
framed = im2double(frame);

% Kliknemo točko na morju
figure; imshow(framed); title('Klikni na morje');
[x, y] = ginput(1);
x = round(x); y = round(y);
close;

% Izrežemo patch okoli klika (morje)
r = 50;
[H_img, W_img, ~] = size(framed);
x1 = max(1, x-r); x2 = min(W_img, x+r);
y1 = max(1, y-r); y2 = min(H_img, y+r);

% učenje barv morja
patch = framed(y1:y2, x1:x2, :);  

% Razdelimo vsak kanal R,G,B na 8 delov ali 8x8x8
bins = 8;
%Definiramo robove bina oziroma bin 1 je na intervalu [0,0.125], bin 2 na [0.125,0.25]
edges = linspace(0,1,bins+1);
% Dolocimo 8x8x8 matrika H oziroma histograma ki ima 0
H = zeros(bins,bins,bins);

% 3D RGB histogram patcha
R = patch(:,:,1); G = patch(:,:,2); B = patch(:,:,3);
% Pove v katerem binu se nahaja piksel iz patcha glede na edges
ri = discretize(R,edges);
gi = discretize(G,edges);
bi = discretize(B,edges);

%Steje piksle, koliko pikslov se pojavijo v posamezen bin
sz = size(patch,1)*size(patch,2);
for i=1:sz
    H(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) = H(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) + 1;
end

% Normalizacija histograma na [0,255]
H = H / max(H(:));
H = H * 255;

% Gremo čez cel video in vsako točko preslikamo skozi histogram
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
writer = VideoWriter('ladja_Naloga4.avi');  
open(writer);

while hasFrame(v)
    f = im2double(readFrame(v));
    R = f(:,:,1); G = f(:,:,2); B = f(:,:,3);

    ri = discretize(R,edges);
    gi = discretize(G,edges);
    bi = discretize(B,edges);

    M = zeros(size(R));          % izhodna sivinska slika
    for i=1:numel(R)
        M(i) = H(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i));   % vrednost iz histograma
    end

    M = uint8(M);
    writeVideo(writer, M);       % zapišemo frame
end

close(writer);
==== END Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_21505_assignsubmission_file | Naloga4_Vaja3.m ====

==== BEGIN Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_21505_assignsubmission_file | Naloga5_Vaja3.m ====
%% Naloga 5 - Segmentacija z uporabo histogram intersection

close all; clear; clc;

% Preberemo prvi frame iz videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = readFrame(v);
framed = im2double(frame);

%Izbere pravokotnik nad morjem (nas patch)
figure; imshow(framed);
title('Selektiraj pravokotnik cez morja');
patch_rect = round(getrect); % [x, y, width, height]

% Koordinate pravokotnika
px = patch_rect(1);  
py = patch_rect(2);
pw = patch_rect(3);  
ph = patch_rect(4);

% Izrežemo patch morja iz slike
patch = framed(py:py+ph, px:px+pw, :);

% Razdelimo R,G,B kanale na 4 delov, kar bo nas referencni histogram
bins = 4;                                   
edges = linspace(0,1,bins+1); 
% prazen 3D histogram 4x4x4
H_ref = zeros(bins,bins,bins);           

% Kanali patcha (morje)
R = patch(:,:,1); G = patch(:,:,2); B = patch(:,:,3);

% Za vsak piksel določimo, v kateri bin
ri = discretize(R, edges);
gi = discretize(G, edges);
bi = discretize(B, edges);

% Polnimo 3D histogram: štejemo, kolikokrat se pojavi kombinacija (ri,gi,bi)
for i = 1:numel(R)
    H_ref(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) = H_ref(ri(i), gi(i), bi(i)) + 1;
end

% Normaliziramo referenčni histogram
H_ref = H_ref / sum(H_ref(:));

% Priprava videa
v = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
writer = VideoWriter('ladja_morje_Naloga5.avi');
open(writer);

block = 8;   % velikost bloka: 8×8 pikslov

% Gremo čez vse frame-e videa
while hasFrame(v)
    frame = im2double(readFrame(v));
    [h, w, ~] = size(frame);

    % izhodna sivinska mapa podobnosti z morjem
    M = zeros(h, w);  

    % Premikamo se po sliki v korakih 8 px po y in x
    for y = 1:block:(h - mod(h,block))
        for x = 1:block:(w - mod(w,block))

            % Izrežemo trenutni blok velikosti 8x8
            blk = frame(y:y+block-1, x:x+block-1, :);

            %Izračun histograma za ta blok (Hb) 
            Hb = zeros(bins,bins,bins);    

            Rb = blk(:,:,1);
            Gb = blk(:,:,2);
            Bb = blk(:,:,3);

            % Določimo indekse binov za kanal R,G,B v bloku
            rbi = discretize(Rb, edges);
            gbi = discretize(Gb, edges);
            bbi = discretize(Bb, edges);

            % Polnimo histogram bloka: štejemo pojavitve kombinacij (rbi,gbi,bbi)
            for i = 1:numel(Rb)
                Hb(rbi(i), gbi(i), bbi(i)) = Hb(rbi(i), gbi(i), bbi(i)) + 1;
            end

            % Normaliziramo histogram bloka, če ni prazen (vsota = 1)
            if sum(Hb(:)) > 0
                Hb = Hb / sum(Hb(:));
            end

            % Histogram intersection (Swain & Ballard)
             % HI meri podobnost med histogramom morja (H_ref) in histogramom trenutnega bloka (Hb)
            HI = sum( min(H_ref(:), Hb(:)) );

            % Preslikamo HI v interval 0–255 za sivinsko sliko
            HI_val = uint8(HI * 256);

            % Celoten blok dobi isto sivinsko vrednost (HI_val)
            % Svetel blok - barve podobne morju, temen blok - drugačne barve
            M(y:y+block-1, x:x+block-1) = HI_val;
        end
    end

    % Zapišemo sivinski frame v video
    writeVideo(writer, uint8(M));
end

close(writer);

disp('konec');
==== END Mihaela Temelkova | Mihaela Temelkova_21505_assignsubmission_file | Naloga5_Vaja3.m ====


==== BEGIN Nejc Ropič | Nejc Ropič_21494_assignsubmission_file | izracun_histograma.m ====
function [N,edgesR,edgesG,edgesB] = izracun_histograma(bins, R, G, B)

    R_norm = double(R(:)) / 255;
    G_norm = double(G(:)) / 255;
    B_norm = double(B(:)) / 255;

    edges = linspace(0,1,bins+1);

    idxR = discretize(R_norm, edges);
    idxG = discretize(G_norm, edges);
    idxB = discretize(B_norm, edges);

    valid = ~isnan(idxR) & ~isnan(idxG) & ~isnan(idxB);

    N = accumarray([idxR(valid), idxG(valid), idxB(valid)], 1, [bins bins bins]);

    edgesR = edges;
    edgesG = edges;
    edgesB = edges;
end
==== END Nejc Ropič | Nejc Ropič_21494_assignsubmission_file | izracun_histograma.m ====

function izrisi_sliko(vrednost, naslov)
    figure;
    imagesc(vrednost);
    colormap(gray);
    colorbar;
    axis image;
    title(naslov);
end

function pretvori_hsv(slika, komponenta, vrednost)
    figure
    A = imread(slika);
    lab = rgb2hsv(A);
    lab(:,:,komponenta) = vrednost;
    rgb = hsv2rgb(lab);
    imshow(rgb)
end

==== BEGIN Nejc Ropič | Nejc Ropič_21494_assignsubmission_file | vaja4.m ====
clc, clear;
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(video,[1, Inf]); 
[height, width, channels, num_of_frames] =size(frames);

% okvir za učenje barve morja
frame = 40;
slika = frames(275,:,:,frame); 

R = slika(:,:,1);
G = slika(:,:,2);
B = slika(:,:,3);

% izracun histograma
[N,edgesR,edgesG,edgesB] = izracun_histograma(8,R,G,B);

% Normalizacija histograma na 0,255
max_bin = max(N(:));
N_norm = uint8( (N / max_bin) * 255 );

% iskanje najbolj polnega bina po normalizaciji
[max_val, idx] = max(N(:));
[iR, iG, iB] = ind2sub(size(N_norm), idx);

% meje bina
R_min = edgesR(iR);
R_max = edgesR(iR+1);

G_min = edgesG(iG);
G_max = edgesG(iG+1);

B_min = edgesB(iB);
B_max = edgesB(iB+1);

% segmentacija slike po barvi
R_all = double(frames(:,:,1,frame))/255;
G_all = double(frames(:,:,2,frame))/255;
B_all = double(frames(:,:,3,frame))/255;

mask = (R_all >= R_min & R_all < R_max & ...
        G_all >= G_min & G_all < G_max & ...
        B_all >= B_min & B_all < B_max);

% prikaz segmentacije
figure;
subplot(1,2,1); imshow(frames(:,:,:,frame)); title('Izvirni frame');
subplot(1,2,2); imshow(mask); title('Segmentacija morja');

%% drugi del
writer = VideoWriter('ladja_sivinska_8bit.avi');
open(writer);

for f = 1:num_of_frames
    
    frameRGB = frames(:,:,:,f);
    
    % normalizerani kanali
    R_all = double(frameRGB(:,:,1)) / 255;
    G_all = double(frameRGB(:,:,2)) / 255;
    B_all = double(frameRGB(:,:,3)) / 255;
    
    % piksli v vektorjih
    R_vec = R_all(:);
    G_vec = G_all(:);
    B_vec = B_all(:);
    
    % ugotavljanje binov
    idxR = discretize(R_vec, edgesR);
    idxG = discretize(G_vec, edgesG);
    idxB = discretize(B_vec, edgesB);
    
    % veljavni piksli
    valid = ~isnan(idxR) & ~isnan(idxG) & ~isnan(idxB);
    
    % vektor za izhodno sivinsko sliko
    gray_vec = zeros(size(R_vec), 'uint8');
    
    % vrednosti iz histograma v vektor
    gray_vec(valid) = N_norm(sub2ind(size(N_norm), idxR(valid), idxG(valid), idxB(valid)));
    
    % pretvorba v 2d sliko
    gray_img = reshape(gray_vec, height, width);
    
    % zapis v video
    writeVideo(writer, gray_img);
end

close(writer);

%% nov video
video_rgb = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
video_gray = VideoReader('ladja_sivinska_8bit.avi');

figure;
while hasFrame(video_gray) && hasFrame(video_rgb)
    frame_rgb = readFrame(video_rgb);
    frame_gray = readFrame(video_gray);

    subplot(1,2,1); imshow(frame_rgb); title('Originalni video');
    subplot(1,2,2); imshow(frame_gray, []); title('Sivinski video (histogram)');
    
    pause(1/video_rgb.FrameRate);
end

==== BEGIN Nejc Ropič | Nejc Ropič_21494_assignsubmission_file | vaja5.m ====
clc; clear;

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frames = read(video,[1, Inf]); 
[height, width, ~, num_frames] = size(frames);

% okvir za učenje barve morja
frame = 40;
slika = frames(275:end,:,:,frame);

% model histograma morja 4×4×4
[H_model, edgesR, edgesG, edgesB] = izracun_histograma(4, slika(:,:,1), slika(:,:,2), slika(:,:,3));

% model normaliziramo na verjetnostno porazdelitev
H_model = double(H_model);
H_model = H_model / sum(H_model(:));

writer = VideoWriter('ladja_sivinska_4bit.avi');
open(writer);

block = 8;

for f = 1:num_frames
    
    frameRGB = frames(:,:,:,f);
    out = zeros(height, width, 'uint8');

    for i = 1:block:height-block+1
        for j = 1:block:width-block+1

            blok = frameRGB(i:i+block-1, j:j+block-1, :);

            % histogram bloka
            [H_block, ~, ~, ~] = izracun_histograma(4, blok(:,:,1), blok(:,:,2), blok(:,:,3));
            H_block = double(H_block);
            
            if sum(H_block(:)) == 0
                s = 0;
            else
                % normalizacija
                s = sum(min(H_model(:), H_block(:)));
            end
            
            % preslikava na [0,255]
            s_norm = uint8(round(255 * s));


            % zapis enake vrednosti za cel blok
            out(i:i+block-1, j:j+block-1) = s_norm;
        end
    end

    writeVideo(writer, out);
end

close(writer);

%% nov video
video_rgb = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
video_gray = VideoReader('ladja_sivinska_4bit.avi');

figure;
while hasFrame(video_gray) && hasFrame(video_rgb)
    frame_rgb = readFrame(video_rgb);
    frame_gray = readFrame(video_gray);

    subplot(1,2,1); imshow(frame_rgb); title('Originalni video');
    subplot(1,2,2); imshow(frame_gray, []); title('Sivinski video (histogram)');
    
    pause(1/video_rgb.FrameRate);
end

%% Naloga 1 – Barvni prostori HSV in Lab
clc; clear; close all;

% Slike iz naloge
slike = { ...
    'yosemite_meadows.jpg', ...
    'coloured_pencils.jpg' ...
};

for idx = 1:length(slike)
    img = imread(slike{idx});
    img_name = slike{idx};
    img_name = img_name(1:end-4);

    fprintf("Obdelujem sliko: %s\n", slike{idx});

    % --- HSV PRETVORBA ---
    hsv_img = rgb2hsv(img);

    H = hsv_img(:,:,1);
    S = hsv_img(:,:,2);
    V = hsv_img(:,:,3);

    % Prikaz HSV komponent
    fig = figure('Name',['HSV - ' slike{idx}], ...
             'Position',[50 50 1600 500]);

    tiledlayout(1,3,'TileSpacing','compact','Padding','compact');

    nexttile; imagesc(H); axis image off; colormap(gray);
    title('H komponenta');

    nexttile; imagesc(S); axis image off; colormap(gray);
    title('S komponenta');

    nexttile; imagesc(V); axis image off; colormap(gray);
    title('V komponenta');

    filename = "1." + img_name + "_HSV.png";
    exportgraphics(fig, filename, 'Resolution',300);


    % --- Lab PRETVORBA ---
    lab_img = rgb2lab(img);

    L = lab_img(:,:,1);
    a = lab_img(:,:,2);
    b = lab_img(:,:,3);

    % Normalizacija za prikaz (Lab ni v [0,1])
    Ln = mat2gray(L);   % samodejno skalarira v 0–1
    an = mat2gray(a);
    bn = mat2gray(b);

    fig = figure('Name',['Lab - ' slike{idx}], ...
             'Position',[50 50 1600 500]);

    tiledlayout(1,3,'TileSpacing','compact','Padding','compact');

    nexttile;imagesc(Ln); axis image off; colormap(gray);
    title('L komponenta');

    nexttile;imagesc(an); axis image off; colormap(gray);
    title('a komponenta');

    nexttile;imagesc(bn); axis image off; colormap(gray);
    title('b komponenta');

    filename = "1."+ img_name + "_LAB.png";
    exportgraphics(fig, filename, 'Resolution',300);
end

%% Naloga 2 – Vpliv posameznih komponent na rekonstruirano RGB sliko
clc; clear; close all;

% Slike iz naloge
slike = { ...
    'yosemite_meadows.jpg', ...
    'coloured_pencils.jpg' ...
};

for idx = 1:length(slike)
    img = im2double(imread(slike{idx}));
    img_name = string(slike{idx}(1:end-4));

    fprintf("Obdelujem sliko: %s\n", slike{idx});

    % ---------------- HSV PROSTOR ----------------
    hsv_img = rgb2hsv(img);

    H = hsv_img(:,:,1);
    S = hsv_img(:,:,2);
    V = hsv_img(:,:,3);

    % Konstante iz navodil:
    constH = 0.5;     % ali mean(H(:))
    constS = 0.5;
    constV = 0.5;

    % Rekonstrukcije
    hsv_H = hsv_img; hsv_H(:,:,1) = constH;
    img_H = hsv2rgb(hsv_H);

    hsv_S = hsv_img; hsv_S(:,:,2) = constS;
    img_S = hsv2rgb(hsv_S);

    hsv_V = hsv_img; hsv_V(:,:,3) = constV;
    img_V = hsv2rgb(hsv_V);

    % Prikaz HSV rezultatov
    fig = figure('Name', ['Vpliv HSV komponent - ' slike{idx}]);

    subplot(2,2,1), imshow(img), title('Original');
    subplot(2,2,2), imshow(img_H), title('H = konst');
    subplot(2,2,3), imshow(img_S), title('S = konst');
    subplot(2,2,4), imshow(img_V), title('V = konst');

    filename = append("2.", img_name, "_HSV.png");
    saveas(fig, filename)

    % ---------------- Lab PROSTOR ----------------
    lab_img = rgb2lab(img);

    L = lab_img(:,:,1);
    a = lab_img(:,:,2);
    b = lab_img(:,:,3);

    % Konstante – po navodilih so dovoljeni poljubni (srednje vrednosti)
    constL = 50;     % L je 0–100
    consta = 0;              % a v [-128, 127]
    constb = 0;              % b v [-128, 127]

    % Rekonstrukcije
    lab_L = lab_img; lab_L(:,:,1) = constL;
    img_L = lab2rgb(lab_L);

    lab_a = lab_img; lab_a(:,:,2) = consta;
    img_a = lab2rgb(lab_a);

    lab_b = lab_img; lab_b(:,:,3) = constb;
    img_b = lab2rgb(lab_b);

    % Prikaz Lab rezultatov
    fig = figure('Name', ['Vpliv Lab komponent - ' slike{idx}]);

    subplot(2,2,1), imshow(img), title('Original');
    subplot(2,2,2), imshow(img_L), title('L = konst');
    subplot(2,2,3), imshow(img_a), title('a = konst');
    subplot(2,2,4), imshow(img_b), title('b = konst');

    filename = append("2.", img_name, "_Lab.png");
    saveas(fig, filename)
end


%% Naloga 3 – Ročna segmentacija na podlagi barve
clc; clear; close all;

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

frame = read(video, 41); % Frame z največ vode
frame_HSV = im2double(frame);

figure; imshow(frame_HSV);
title('Izbrani frame za analizo barv');

% --- Omogoči prikaz RGB ob kliku na piksel ---
dcm = datacursormode(gcf);
dcm.Enable = 'on';
dcm.UpdateFcn = @(obj,event) myClickCallback(event, frame_HSV);

% --- Callback funkcija (MORA BITI NA KONCU DATOTEKE) ---
function l = myClickCallback(event, img)
    pos = round(event.Position);
    x = pos(1);
    y = pos(2);
    R = img(y,x,1);
    G = img(y,x,2);
    B = img(y,x,3);

    fprintf("Klik na piksel (%d, %d): R=%.3f  G=%.3f  B=%.3f\n", ...
        x, y, R, G, B);
    l = true;
end


%% Določevanje moraj RGB
Rmin = 0.10;  Rmax = 0.30;
Gmin = 0.15;  Gmax = 0.40;
Bmin = 0.10;  Bmax = 0.35;

output_RGB = VideoWriter('3.segmentacija_RGB.avi');
output_RGB.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(output_RGB);

video.CurrentTime = 0;

while hasFrame(video)
    frm = im2double(readFrame(video));

    R = frm(:,:,1);
    G = frm(:,:,2);
    B = frm(:,:,3);

    mask = (R > Rmin & R < Rmax & ...
            G > Gmin & G < Gmax & ...
            B > Bmin & B < Bmax);

    bin_img = uint8(mask) * 255;  % črno-beli prikaz

    writeVideo(output_RGB, bin_img);
end

close(output_RGB);
fprintf("segmentacija_RGB.avi ustvarjen.\n");

%% Pridobivanje HSV moraj
clc;
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

frame = read(video, 41); % Frame z največ vode

% --- Callback funkcija (MORA BITI NA KONCU DATOTEKE) ---
function l = myClickCallbackHSV(event, img)
    pos = round(event.Position);
    x = pos(1);
    y = pos(2);
    H = img(y,x,1);
    S = img(y,x,2);
    V = img(y,x,3);

    fprintf("Klik na piksel (%d, %d): H=%.3f  S=%.3f  V=%.3f\n", ...
        x, y, H, S, V);
    l = true;
end
%% Določevanje moraj RGB
Hmin = 0.32;  Hmax = 0.46;
Smin = 0.15;  Smax = 0.60;
Vmin = 0.25;  Vmax = 0.45;

output_HSV = VideoWriter('3.segmentacija_HSV.avi');
output_HSV.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(output_HSV);

video.CurrentTime = 0;

while hasFrame(video)
    frm = rgb2hsv(im2double(readFrame(video)));

    H = frm(:,:,1);
    S = frm(:,:,2);
    V = frm(:,:,3);

    mask = (H > Hmin & H < Hmax & ...
            S > Smin & S < Smax & ...
            V > Vmin & V < Vmax);

    bin_img = uint8(mask) * 255;  % črno-beli prikaz

    writeVideo(output_HSV, bin_img);
end

close(output_HSV);
fprintf("segmentacija_HSV.avi ustvarjen.\n");
%% Naloga 4 – Avtomatska segmentacija
clc; clear; close all;

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = im2double(read(video, 41));

figure; imshow(frame);
title('Izberite pravokotni ROI za morje');

roi = drawrectangle();
mask = roi.createMask(frame);   % 2D logična maska

% Izrez "patcha" za histogram
[y, x] = find(mask);
patch = frame(min(y):max(y), min(x):max(x), :);

% figure; imshow(patch);
title('Izrezana tipična barva morja');

% --- 2) Izračun 3D RGB histograma nxnxn ---
for bins = [2, 4, 6, 8, 16]
    R = patch(:,:,1);
    G = patch(:,:,2);
    B = patch(:,:,3);

    % Indeksi za vsak kanal
    Ri = floor(R * bins) + 1;  Ri(Ri > bins) = bins;
    Gi = floor(G * bins) + 1;  Gi(Gi > bins) = bins;
    Bi = floor(B * bins) + 1;  Bi(Bi > bins) = bins;

    hist3D = zeros(bins, bins, bins);

    for i = 1:numel(Ri)
        r = Ri(i); g = Gi(i); b = Bi(i);
        hist3D(r, g, b) = hist3D(r, g, b) + 1;
    end

    % --- 3) Normalizacija histograma ---
    max_val = max(hist3D(:));
    if max_val > 0
        hist3D = hist3D / max_val * 255;
    end

    % --- 4) Uporaba histograma za obdelavo videa ---
    video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
    filename = append('4.segmentacija_histogram_' , string(bins), '.avi');
    output = VideoWriter(filename);
    output.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
    open(output);

    while hasFrame(video)
        frm = im2double(readFrame(video));

        Rf = frm(:,:,1);
        Gf = frm(:,:,2);
        Bf = frm(:,:,3);

        % Indeksi binov
        Ri = floor(Rf * bins) + 1; Ri(Ri > bins) = bins;
        Gi = floor(Gf * bins) + 1; Gi(Gi > bins) = bins;
        Bi = floor(Bf * bins) + 1; Bi(Bi > bins) = bins;

        % Sivinska slika, kjer vsak piksel dobi vrednost histograma
        gray_img = zeros(size(Rf));

        for y = 1:size(frm,1)
            for x = 1:size(frm,2)
                r = Ri(y,x);
                g = Gi(y,x);
                b = Bi(y,x);
                gray_img(y,x) = hist3D(r,g,b);
            end
        end

        gray_img = uint8(gray_img);

        writeVideo(output, gray_img);
    end
    close(output)
    fig = figure; hold on;

    for r = 1:bins
        for g = 1:bins
            for b = 1:bins
                v = hist3D(r,g,b);   % vrednost
                if v > 0
                    scatter3(r, g, b, v/2, [r g b] / bins, 'filled');
                end
            end
        end
    end
    grid on;
    view(45, 30);
    xlabel('R bin');
    ylabel('G bin');
    zlabel('B bin');
    title('3D RGB histogram');
    figurename = append('4.segmentacija_histogram_' , string(bins), '.png');
    saveas(fig, figurename)
    rotate3d on;
end


%% Naloga 5 – Histogram intersection segmentacija (4x4x4 + bloki 8x8)
clc; clear; close all;

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

frame = im2double(read(video, 41));
figure; imshow(frame);
title('Izberite pravokotnik, ki predstavlja morje');
rectangle = drawrectangle();

mask = rectangle.createMask(frame);
[y, x] = find(mask);
selected_part_of_frame = frame(min(y):max(y), min(x):max(x), :);

% --- 2) Izračun referenčnega 4×4×4 histograma ---
bins = 4;
hist_ref = rgb_histogram(selected_part_of_frame, bins);
norm_hist_ref = hist_ref / sum(hist_ref);

fig = figure; bar(norm_hist_ref);
title('Referenčni histogram morja (4×4×4)');

saveas(fig, "5.referecni_diagram.png");

% --- 3) Obdelava videa v blokih 8×8 ---
video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
out = VideoWriter('5.segmentacija_intersection.avi');
out.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(out);

block_size = 8;

while hasFrame(video)
    frm = im2double(readFrame(video));
    [h, w, ~] = size(frm);
    out_img = zeros(h, w);

    for row = 1:block_size:h-block_size+1
        for col = 1:block_size:w-block_size+1

            block = frm(row:row+block_size-1, col:col+block_size-1, :);

            hist_blk = rgb_histogram(block, bins);
            hist_blk = hist_blk / sum(hist_blk);

            % I = sum(min(norm_hist_ref, hist_blk));
            I = sum(min(hist_ref, hist_blk)) / sum(hist_ref);

            out_img(row:row+block_size-1, col:col+block_size-1) = I * 256;
        end
    end

    writeVideo(out, uint8(out_img));
end

close(out);

fprintf("segmentacija_intersection.avi ustvarjen.\n");

function H = rgb_histogram(img, bins)
    img = im2double(img);
    R = img(:,:,1); G = img(:,:,2); B = img(:,:,3);

    Ri = floor(R * bins) + 1; Ri(Ri > bins) = bins;
    Gi = floor(G * bins) + 1; Gi(Gi > bins) = bins;
    Bi = floor(B * bins) + 1; Bi(Bi > bins) = bins;

    H3 = zeros(bins, bins, bins);

    for i = 1:numel(Ri)
        H3(Ri(i), Gi(i), Bi(i)) = H3(Ri(i), Gi(i), Bi(i)) + 1;
    end

    H = H3(:);   % 64-dim vektor
end

R_obMorjaMax = max(R_obMorja(:));

   maskRGB = (R >= R_obMorjaMin & R <= R_obMorjaMax) & ...
              (G >= G_obMorjaMin & G <= G_obMorjaMax) & ...
              (B >= B_obMorjaMin & B <= B_obMorjaMax);

   RGBframe = uint8(maskRGB)*255; %ce ni morje = 0 crno, ce je morje = 255 belo
   writeVideo(rezVideo,RGBframe);

%% Naloga 4 – Segmentacija na podlagi barve (3D RGB histogram)

Video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
okvirSlike = 50;                    % isti okvir kot v nalogi 3
Okvir = read(Video, okvirSlike);

figure;
imshow(Okvir);
title('Izbrana slika za dolocanje obmocja morja (RGB)');

% Izberemo obmocje, kjer je samo morje
[visina, sirina, ~] = size(Okvir);
y_start = round(5*visina/6);
y_end   = visina;
x_end   = round(sirina/2);

obmocjeMorja = Okvir(y_start:y_end, 1:x_end, :);

% Izracun 3D histograma iz obmocja morja

bine   = 8;               % 8 binov na vsaki barvni osi
binSize = 256 / bine;     % = 32

% posamezni barvni kanali
R = obmocjeMorja(:,:,1);
G = obmocjeMorja(:,:,2);
B = obmocjeMorja(:,:,3);

% vektorji (1D) vseh pikslov
Rv = R(:);
Gv = G(:);
Bv = B(:);

% indeksi binov za vsak piksel
rBin = floor(Rv / binSize) + 1;
gBin = floor(Gv / binSize) + 1;
bBin = floor(Bv / binSize) + 1;

% pazimo, da ne gremo preko st binov (zaradi vrednosti 255)
rBin(rBin > bine) = bine;
gBin(gBin > bine) = bine;
bBin(bBin > bine) = bine;

% inicializacija 3D histograma
H3D = zeros(bine, bine, bine);

% napolnimo 3D histogram: stevilo pikslov v vsakem 3D binu
for i = 1:length(Rv)
    H3D(rBin(i), gBin(i), bBin(i)) = H3D(rBin(i), gBin(i), bBin(i)) + 1;
end

% 4) Normalizacija histograma na [0, 255]

maxVal = max(H3D(:));
H3D_norm = H3D * (255 / maxVal);  % skaliramo da bi imelli max vrednost 255(kolko krat se pojavi vrednost ene celice)
H3D_norm = uint8(H3D_norm);          % da dobimo svetlost 0–255

% Obdelava celotnega videa z uporabo 3D histograma

vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
genVid = VideoWriter('ladja_morjeHistogramNaloga4.avi');
open(genVid);

while hasFrame(vid)
    frame = readFrame(vid);        % originalni RGB frame

    Rf = frame(:,:,1);
    Gf = frame(:,:,2);
    Bf = frame(:,:,3);

    % bin indeksi za vsak piksel v trenutnem framu
    rBinF = floor(Rf / binSize) + 1;
    gBinF = floor(Gf / binSize) + 1;
    bBinF = floor(Bf / binSize) + 1;

    rBinF(rBinF > bine) = bine;
    gBinF(gBinF > bine) = bine;
    bBinF(bBinF > bine) = bine;

    [h, w, ~] = size(frame);
    grayFrame = zeros(h, w);       % tukaj bomo shranili sivinsko sliko

    % za vsak piksel vzamemo vrednost iz normiranega 3D histograma
    for y = 1:h
        for x = 1:w
            grayFrame(y,x) = H3D_norm( rBinF(y,x), gBinF(y,x), bBinF(y,x) ); % npr. grayFrame(y,x) = 230, oz. vzamemo vrednost klk norm. pixlov so na poziciji H3D_norm(3,5,4)_ kje je tud pixel in mu prepisemo vrendnost na tega piksla v frameu
        end
    end

    grayFrame = uint8(grayFrame);  % 0–255 sivinska slika
    writeVideo(genVid, grayFrame);
end

close(genVid);

%% Naloga 5

Video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
okvirSlike = 50;                    % isti okvir
Okvir = read(Video, okvirSlike);

figure;
imshow(Okvir);
title('Izbrana slika za dolocanje obmocja morja (RGB)');

% Izberemo obmocje, kjer je samo morje
[visina, sirina, ~] = size(Okvir);
y_start = round(5*visina/6);
y_end   = visina;
x_end   = round(sirina/2);

obmocjeMorja = Okvir(y_start:y_end, 1:x_end, :);

% Izracun 3D histograma iz obmocja morja

bine = 4;               % 4 binov na vsaki barvni osi
binSize = 256 / bine;     

% posamezni barvni kanali v double
R = obmocjeMorja(:,:,1);
G = obmocjeMorja(:,:,2);
B = obmocjeMorja(:,:,3);

% vektorji (1D) vseh pikslov
Rv = R(:);
Gv = G(:);
Bv = B(:);

% indeksi binov za vsak piksel
rBins = floor(Rv / binSize) + 1;
gBins = floor(Gv / binSize) + 1;
bBins = floor(Bv / binSize) + 1;

% pazimo, da ne gremo preko nbins (zaradi vrednosti 255)
rBins(rBins > bine) = bine;
gBins(gBins > bine) = bine;
bBins(bBins > bine) = bine;

H_model = zeros(bine,bine,bine);

for i = 1:length(Rv)
    H_model(rBins(i), gBins(i), bBins(i)) = H_model(rBins(i), gBins(i), bBins(i)) + 1;
end

H_modelNorm = H_model / sum(H_model(:));

vid = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
out = VideoWriter('ladja_morjeNaloga5HistRazd.avi');
open(out);

blockSize = 8;

while hasFrame(vid)
    frame = readFrame(vid);
    [H, W, ~] = size(frame);
    grayOut = zeros(H, W);

    % blokovi 8×8
    for y = 1:blockSize:H-blockSize+1
        for x = 1:blockSize:W-blockSize+1

            block = frame(y:y+blockSize-1, x:x+blockSize-1, :);

            % histogram bloka
            Hb = zeros(bine,bine,bine);

            Rb = block(:,:,1); Gb = block(:,:,2); Bb = block(:,:,3);
            Rbv = Rb(:); Gbv = Gb(:); Bbv = Bb(:);

            rB = floor(Rbv/binSize)+1; rB(rB>bine)=bine;
            gB = floor(Gbv/binSize)+1; gB(gB>bine)=bine;
            bB = floor(Bbv/binSize)+1; bB(bB>bine)=bine;

            for k = 1:length(Rbv)
                Hb(rB(k), gB(k), bB(k)) = Hb(rB(k), gB(k), bB(k)) + 1;
            end

            Hb_norm = Hb / sum(Hb(:));

            % histogram intersection
            HI = sum(min(H_modelNorm(:), Hb_norm(:)))/sum(H_modelNorm(:));

            % pretvorimo v 0–255 območje
            value = uint8(256 * HI);

            % napolnimo cel blok s to vrednostjo
            grayOut(y:y+blockSize-1, x:x+blockSize-1) = value;

        end
    end

    grayOut = uint8(grayOut);
    writeVideo(out, grayOut);
end

close(out);

% vaja3.m

close all; clear; clc;

% Testne datoteke
img1_url = 'http://vision.fe.uni-lj.si/classes/RV/vaje/vaja3/yosemite_meadows.jpg';
img2_url = 'http://vision.fe.uni-lj.si/classes/RV/vaje/vaja3/coloured_pencils.jpg';
video_url = 'http://vision.fe.uni-lj.si/classes/RV/vaje/vaja3/ladja.avi';

% Izhodna mapa
outdir = 'rezultati';
if ~exist(outdir,'dir'), mkdir(outdir); end

%% Naloga 1: Pretvorba v HSV in Lab, prikaz komponent
fprintf('Naloga 1: nalaganje in pretvorbe (HSV, Lab)...\n');
I1 = im2double(imread(img1_url));
I2 = im2double(imread(img2_url));

sivinske_komponente(I1,'yosemite',outdir);
sivinske_komponente(I2,'pencils',outdir);

%% Naloga 2: Učinek izločanja posameznih komponent (HSV in Lab)
fprintf('Naloga 2: vpliv posameznih komponent (HSV in Lab)...\n');

komponente(I1,'yosemite',outdir);
komponente(I2,'pencils',outdir);

%% Naloga 3: Ročna segmentacija na podlagi barve (RGB in HSV)
fprintf('Naloga 3: ročna segmentacija (RGB in HSV) na videu ladja.avi ...\n');

try
    vfile = websave(fullfile(outdir,'ladja.avi'), video_url);
catch
    vfile = video_url;
end
manual_color_segmentation(vfile, outdir);

%% Naloga 4: Avtomatska segmentacija s 3D RGB histogramom
fprintf('Naloga 4: avtomatska segmentacija z 3D RGB histogramom ...\n');
auto_hist_segmentation(vfile, outdir);

%% Naloga 5: Segmentacija na podlagi preseka histogramov (4x4x4) po blokih 8x8
fprintf('Naloga 5: segmentacija na podlagi razdalje med histogrami (block 8x8) ...\n');
hist_intersection_block_segmentation(vfile, outdir);

fprintf('Vse izvedeno. Rezultati so v mapi "%s".\n', outdir);

%% Lokalne funkcije
function sivinske_komponente(I,basename,outdir)
    % I: double RGB [0..1]
    hsv = rgb2hsv(I);
    lab = rgb2lab(I); % L in [0..100], a,b approx [-128..127]
    figure('Name',['Components: ' basename],'Visible','off');
    
    % Sive HSV komponente
    subplot(2,3,1), imagesc(hsv(:,:,1)); axis image off; colormap gray; title('H');
    subplot(2,3,2), imagesc(hsv(:,:,2)); axis image off; colormap gray; title('S');
    subplot(2,3,3), imagesc(hsv(:,:,3)); axis image off; colormap gray; title('V');
    
    % Sive Lab komponente
    subplot(2,3,4), imagesc(lab(:,:,1)/100); axis image off; colormap gray; title('L');
    subplot(2,3,5), imagesc((lab(:,:,2)+128)/256); axis image off; colormap gray; title('a');
    subplot(2,3,6), imagesc((lab(:,:,3)+128)/256); axis image off; colormap gray; title('b');
    
    saveas(gcf, fullfile(outdir,[basename '_components.png']));
    close;
end

function komponente(I,basename,outdir)
    % Prikaže vpliv nastavitve komponente na konstanto za HSV in Lab
    hsv = rgb2hsv(I);
    lab = rgb2lab(I);
    % HSV: za vsako komponento privzemi sredinsko vrednost in rekonstruiraj
    mids_hsv = [0.5, 0.5, 0.5];
    for k=1:3
        tmp = hsv;
        tmp(:,:,k) = mids_hsv(k);
        recon = hsv2rgb(tmp);
        imwrite(recon, fullfile(outdir, sprintf('%s_hsv_fix_%d.png',basename,k)));
    end
    % Lab: nastavimo L, a, b na srednje vrednosti
    % L = 50, a,b = 0
    mids_lab = [50, 0, 0];
    for k=1:3
        tmp = lab;
        tmp(:,:,k) = mids_lab(k);
        recon = lab2rgb(tmp,'OutputType','double');
        recon = min(max(recon,0),1);
        imwrite(recon, fullfile(outdir, sprintf('%s_lab_fix_%d.png',basename,k)));
    end
end

function manual_color_segmentation(vfile,outdir)
    % Ročna segmentacija 
    vr = VideoReader(vfile);
    frame = readFrame(vr);
    % Shrani vzorčni frame
    imwrite(frame, fullfile(outdir,'ladja_sample_frame.png'));
    
    R = frame(:,:,1); G = frame(:,:,2); B = frame(:,:,3);
    % RGB Vrednosti
    Rmin=50; Rmax=120;
    Gmin=75; Gmax=165;
    Bmin=50; Bmax=95;

    % Binarna maska
    mask_rgb = (R>=Rmin & R<=Rmax) & (G>=Gmin & G<=Gmax) & (B>=Bmin & B<=Bmax);
    % Shrani vzorčno masko
    imwrite(mask_rgb, fullfile(outdir,'ladja_manual_mask_sample_rgb.png'));
    % Izgradnja videa
    vr.CurrentTime = 0;
    vw = VideoWriter(fullfile(outdir,'ladja_manual_rgb.avi'));
    open(vw);

    while hasFrame(vr)
        f = readFrame(vr);
        R = f(:,:,1); G=f(:,:,2); B=f(:,:,3);
        mask = (R>=Rmin & R<=Rmax) & (G>=Gmin & G<=Gmax) & (B>=Bmin & B<=Bmax);
        writeVideo(vw,uint8(mask*255));
    end

    close(vw);

    % HSV prostor
    vr = VideoReader(vfile); hsvvr=VideoWriter(fullfile(outdir,'ladja_manual_hsv.avi'));
    open(hsvvr);

    while hasFrame(vr)
        f = im2double(readFrame(vr));
        hsi = rgb2hsv(f);
        H = hsi(:,:,1); S = hsi(:,:,2); V = hsi(:,:,3);
        % HSV vrednosti
        mask_hsv = (H>0.3 & H<0.5) & (S>0.1 & S<1) & (V);
        writeVideo(hsvvr,uint8(mask_hsv*255));
    end

    close(hsvvr);
end

function auto_hist_segmentation(vfile,outdir)
    vr = VideoReader(vfile);
    
    frame = readFrame(vr);
    [h,w,~] = size(frame);

    % ROI
    cx = round(w/2); cy = round(h*0.65); s = min(round(min(h,w)/12),50);
    roi = frame(cy-s:cy+s, cx-s:cx+s, :);
    imwrite(roi, fullfile(outdir,'ladja_hist_roi.png'));

    % Zgradimo 8x8x8 histogram
    bins = 8;

    % Diskretiziraj 0..255 v bin številke 1..bins
    R = double(roi(:,:,1)); G = double(roi(:,:,2)); B = double(roi(:,:,3));
    edges = linspace(0,255,bins+1);
    rbin = discretize(R,edges);
    gbin = discretize(G,edges);
    bbin = discretize(B,edges);
    idx = sub2ind([bins,bins,bins], rbin(:), gbin(:), bbin(:));
    H = accumarray(idx,1,[bins^3,1]);
    H = reshape(H, [bins,bins,bins]);

    % Normaliziraj tako, da je najbolj poln bin = 255
    H = double(H);
    if max(H(:))>0
        H = H / max(H(:)) * 255;
    end

    % Shrani histogram
    save(fullfile(outdir,'ladja_rgb_hist_8x8x8.mat'),'H','edges');
    
    vr.CurrentTime = 0;
    vw = VideoWriter(fullfile(outdir,'ladja_histmap_8x8x8.avi'));
    open(vw);

    while hasFrame(vr)
        f = readFrame(vr);
        R = double(f(:,:,1)); G = double(f(:,:,2)); B = double(f(:,:,3));
        rbin = discretize(R,edges); gbin = discretize(G,edges); bbin = discretize(B,edges);
        % za robne piksle, kjer discretize vrne NaN, nastavimo 1
        rbin(isnan(rbin)) = 1; gbin(isnan(gbin)) = 1; bbin(isnan(bbin)) = 1;
        idx = sub2ind([bins,bins,bins], rbin(:), gbin(:), bbin(:));
        val = H(idx); % vektor dolžine numPix
        imgray = reshape(uint8(val), size(R));
        writeVideo(vw, imgray);
    end
    close(vw);
end

function hist_intersection_block_segmentation(vfile,outdir)
    vr = VideoReader(vfile);
    frame = readFrame(vr);
    [h,w,~] = size(frame);

    % ROI
    cx = round(w/2); cy = round(h*0.65); s = min(round(min(h,w)/12),40);
    roi = frame(cy-s:cy+s, cx-s:cx+s,:);
    imwrite(roi, fullfile(outdir,'ladja_hist4x4x4_roi.png'));
    
    % Histogram 4x4x4
    bins = 4;
    edges = linspace(0,255,bins+1);

    % Histogram iz ROI
    R = double(roi(:,:,1)); G = double(roi(:,:,2)); B = double(roi(:,:,3));
    rbin = discretize(R,edges); gbin = discretize(G,edges); bbin = discretize(B,edges);
    idx = sub2ind([bins,bins,bins], rbin(:), gbin(:), bbin(:));
    Hmodel = accumarray(idx,1,[bins^3,1]);
    Hmodel = double(Hmodel) / sum(Hmodel(:)); % normaliziramo na sum=1

    % Obdelava videa po blokih 8x8
    block = 8;
    vr.CurrentTime = 0;
    vw = VideoWriter(fullfile(outdir,'ladja_hist_intersection_blocks.avi'));
    open(vw);
    while hasFrame(vr)
        f = readFrame(vr);
        [h,w,~] = size(f);
        outFrame = zeros(h,w,'uint8');
        % blok po blok
        nbh = floor(h/block); 
        nbw = floor(w/block);
        
        for by = 1:nbh
            for bx = 1:nbw
                ys = (by-1)*block+1; ye = by*block;
                xs = (bx-1)*block+1; xe = bx*block;
                
                patch = f(ys:ye, xs:xe, :);
                R = double(patch(:,:,1)); G = double(patch(:,:,2)); B = double(patch(:,:,3));
                
                rbin = discretize(R,edges); gbin = discretize(G,edges); bbin = discretize(B,edges);
                rbin(isnan(rbin)) = 1; gbin(isnan(gbin)) = 1; bbin(isnan(bbin)) = 1;
                idx = sub2ind([bins,bins,bins], rbin(:), gbin(:), bbin(:));
                
                Hpatch = accumarray(idx,1,[bins^3,1]);
                Hpatch = double(Hpatch) / sum(Hpatch(:)); % normaliziraj

                % Histogram intersection
                inter = sum(min(Hmodel, Hpatch)) / sum(Hmodel); % normalizirana vrednost med 0 in 1
                val = uint8(inter * 255); % razpon 0..255
                outFrame(ys:ye, xs:xe) = val;

            end
        end
        writeVideo(vw, outFrame);
    end
    close(vw);
end

==== BEGIN Tina Gutnik | Tina Gutnik_21495_assignsubmission_file | konstantne_komponente.m ====
function konstantne_komponente(datoteka)
% vhod datoteke mora biti v obliki 'slika.jpg'
% funkcija vrne 6 rgb slik - v primeru konstantne vsake izmed komponent HSV in Lab

I_int = imread(datoteka);
I_double = im2double(I_int);

% HSV prostor

I_hsv = rgb2hsv(I_double);  % pretvorimo v HSV prostor

I_hsv_H = I_hsv;
I_hsv_H(:,:,1) = 0.5;  % H je konstanten, torej imajo vsi piksli isti odtenek barve

I_hsv_S = I_hsv; 
I_hsv_S(:,:,2) = 0.5;  % S kje konstanten, torej so vse barve enako žive

I_hsv_V = I_hsv; 
I_hsv_V(:,:,3) = 0.5;  % V je konstanten, torej so vsi piksli enako svetli


I_rgb_H = hsv2rgb(I_hsv_H);  % pretvorimo nazaj v RGB prostorž
figure;
imshow(I_rgb_H);
title('HSV: konstanten H');

I_rgb_S = hsv2rgb(I_hsv_S);
figure;
imshow(I_rgb_S);
title('HSV: konstanten S');

I_rgb_V = hsv2rgb(I_hsv_V);
figure;
imshow(I_rgb_V);
title('HSV: konstanten V');

% Lab prostor

I_lab = rgb2lab(I_double);

I_lab_L = I_lab;
I_lab_L(:,:,1) = 50;  % L je konstanten, torej 

I_lab_a = I_lab; 
I_lab_a(:,:,2) = 0;  % a kje konstanten, torej 

I_lab_b = I_lab; 
I_lab_b(:,:,3) = 0;  % b je konstanten, torej


I_rgb_L = lab2rgb(I_lab_L);  % pretvorimo nazaj v RGB prostor
figure;
imshow(I_rgb_L);
title('Lab: konstanten L');

I_rgb_a = lab2rgb(I_lab_a);
figure;
imshow(I_rgb_a);
title('Lab: konstanten a');

I_rgb_b = lab2rgb(I_lab_b);
figure;
imshow(I_rgb_b);
title('Lab: konstanten b');

end

%% 1. naloga

sivinska_slika_komponent('coloured_pencils.jpg');
sivinska_slika_komponent('yosemite_meadows.jpg');

%% 2. naloga

konstantne_komponente('coloured_pencils.jpg');
konstantne_komponente('yosemite_meadows.jpg');

%% 3. naloga

% RGB segmentacija

V = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(V, 20);    % izberemo en frame
%imshow(frame);

R_min = 30; % dobimo s klikanjem pixlov s funkcijo impixel()
R_max = 110;
G_min = 60;
G_max = 130;
B_min = 40;
B_max = 130;

seg_V = VideoWriter('ladja_segmentacija_rgb.avi');
seg_V.FrameRate = V.FrameRate;
open(seg_V);

while hasFrame(V)
    I = readFrame(V);         % en frame gledamo kot sliko uint
    I_double = im2double(I);  

    R = I(:,:,1);
    G = I(:,:,2);
    B = I(:,:,3);

   
    maska =  R >= R_min & R <= R_max & G >= G_min & G <= G_max & B >= B_min & B <= B_max;

    % črnobela slika; morje je belo, vse ostalo je črno; zato predelamo nazaj v uint
    
    frame = uint8(maska) * 255;
    writeVideo(seg_V, frame);
end

close(seg_V);
implay('ladja_segmentacija_rgb.avi');

% HSV segmentacija

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video, 20);
frame_double = im2double(frame);
frame_hsv = rgb2hsv(frame_double);
%imshow(frame_hsv);

H = frame_hsv(:,:,1);
S = frame_hsv(:,:,2);
V = frame_hsv(:,:,3);

H_min = 0.28; 
H_max = 0.47;   
S_min = 0.09;  
S_max = 0.5;    
V_min = 0.26;  
V_max = 0.51;    


seg_video = VideoWriter('ladja_segmentacija_hsv.avi');
seg_video.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(seg_video);

while hasFrame(video)
    I = readFrame(video);        
    I_double = im2double(I);
    I_hsv = rgb2hsv(I_double);

    H = I_hsv(:,:,1);
    S = I_hsv(:,:,2);
    V = I_hsv(:,:,3);

    % lahko bi uporabili samo H ali pa H in S
    maska =  H >= H_min & H <= H_max & S >= S_min & S <= S_max & V >= V_min & V <= V_max;

    frame = uint8(maska) * 255;
    writeVideo(seg_video, frame);
end

close(seg_video);
implay('ladja_segmentacija_hsv.avi');

%% 4. naloga

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video, 20);         
imshow(frame);

izrez = imcrop(frame);      % z miško povlečemo pravokotnik in dvakrat kliknemo znotraj pravokotnika
imshow(izrez);

R = izrez(:,:,1);
G = izrez(:,:,2);
B = izrez(:,:,3);

st_binov = 8;             % število binov na vsaki osi
sirina_bina = 256 / st_binov;  % širina enega bina je 32

H = zeros(st_binov, st_binov, st_binov);  % inicializiramo histogram

[vrstice, stolpci, ~] = size(izrez);

for i = 1:vrstice                % gremo čez vse vrstice in stolpce (torej čez vse piksle) in polnimo histogram
    for j = 1:stolpci
        
        r = double(R(i,j));   % spotoma pretvorimo v double
        g = double(G(i,j));
        b = double(B(i,j));

        r_bin = floor(r / sirina_bina) + 1;  % izračunamo indeks bina za vsak kanal od 1 do 8
        g_bin = floor(g / sirina_bina) + 1;
        b_bin = floor(b / sirina_bina) + 1;

        H(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = H(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;  % povečamo števec v tem binu
    end
end

maks = max(H(:));   % normalizacija: največji vrednosti pripišemo 255
H = H * (255 / maks); 

% pretvorba videa v sivinski posnetek

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

video_siv = VideoWriter('sivinska_ladja_histogram.avi');
video_siv.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(video_siv);

while hasFrame(video)   % gremo čez vse frameje
    frame = readFrame(video);              
    [vrstice_frame, stolpci_frame, ~] = size(frame);
    
    frame_siv = zeros(vrstice_frame, stolpci_frame, 'uint8'); % pripravimo siv frame
    
    R_f = double(frame(:,:,1));
    G_f = double(frame(:,:,2));
    B_f = double(frame(:,:,3));
    
    for i = 1:vrstice_frame          % gremo čez vse piksle znotraj enega frameja 
        for j = 1:stolpci_frame
            
            r = R_f(i,j);
            g = G_f(i,j);
            b = B_f(i,j);
            
            r_bin = floor(r / sirina_bina) + 1;
            g_bin = floor(g / sirina_bina) + 1;
            b_bin = floor(b / sirina_bina) + 1;
            
            siv_H = H(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin);  % preberemo sivinsko vrednost iz norm. histograma
            
            frame_siv(i,j) = siv_H;          % zapišemo v sivinsko sliko
        end
    end
   
    writeVideo(video_siv, frame_siv);           % zapišemo sivinski frame v video
end

close(video_siv);
implay('sivinska_ladja_histogram.avi');

%% 5. naloga

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');
frame = read(video, 20);         
imshow(frame);

izrez = imcrop(frame);      % z miško povlečemo pravokotnik in dvakrat kliknemo znotraj pravokotnika
imshow(izrez);

R = izrez(:,:,1);
G = izrez(:,:,2);
B = izrez(:,:,3);

st_binov = 4;            
sirina_bina = 256 / st_binov;  % širina enega bina je 64

H = zeros(st_binov, st_binov, st_binov);  

[vrstice, stolpci, ~] = size(izrez);

for i = 1:vrstice                
    for j = 1:stolpci
        
        r = double(R(i,j));   
        g = double(G(i,j));
        b = double(B(i,j));

        r_bin = floor(r / sirina_bina) + 1;  
        g_bin = floor(g / sirina_bina) + 1;
        b_bin = floor(b / sirina_bina) + 1;

        H(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = H(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;  
    end
end

H_ref = H(:);   % normalizacija: da je vsota 1
vsota_ref = sum(H_ref);
H_ref = H_ref / vsota_ref;

% pripravimo video

video = VideoReader('ladja.avi');

video_izhod = VideoWriter('ladja_intersection.avi');
video_izhod.FrameRate = video.FrameRate;
open(video_izhod);

velikost_bloka = 8; % 8x8

while hasFrame(video)   % obdelamo vse frameje
    frame = readFrame(video);          
    [vrstice_frame, stolpci_frame, ~] = size(frame);

    bloki_vrstica = floor(vrstice_frame / velikost_bloka);   % poračunamo koliko celih blokov gre v sliko
    bloki_stolpec = floor(stolpci_frame / velikost_bloka);   % tukaj s floor zagotovimo, da ignoriramo rob

    bloki_vrstica_1 = bloki_vrstica * velikost_bloka;         % poračunamo koliko dejanskih blokov je celih notri
    bloki_stolpec_1 = bloki_stolpec * velikost_bloka;
    
    frame_siv = zeros(bloki_vrstica_1, bloki_stolpec_1, 'uint8');  % pripravi siv frame

    R_f = double(frame(:,:,1));
    G_f = double(frame(:,:,2));
    B_f = double(frame(:,:,3));

    for v = 1:bloki_vrstica
        for s = 1:bloki_stolpec

            vrstica_zac = (v - 1) * velikost_bloka + 1;
            vrstica_kon = v * velikost_bloka;
            stolpec_zac = (s - 1) * velikost_bloka + 1;
            stolpec_kon = s * velikost_bloka;

            blok_R = R_f(vrstica_zac:vrstica_kon, stolpec_zac:stolpec_kon); % za vsak kanal izrežemo 8x8 blok
            blok_G = G_f(vrstica_zac:vrstica_kon, stolpec_zac:stolpec_kon);
            blok_B = B_f(vrstica_zac:vrstica_kon, stolpec_zac:stolpec_kon);

            H_blok = zeros(st_binov, st_binov, st_binov);

            for i = 1:velikost_bloka
                for j = 1:velikost_bloka
                    
                    r = blok_R(i,j);
                    g = blok_G(i,j);
                    b = blok_B(i,j);
                    
                    r_bin = floor(r / sirina_bina) + 1;
                    g_bin = floor(g / sirina_bina) + 1;
                    b_bin = floor(b / sirina_bina) + 1;

                    H_blok(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) = H_blok(r_bin, g_bin, b_bin) + 1;
                end
            end

            H_blok_ref = H_blok(:);  % normalizacija
            vsota_blok_ref = sum(H_blok_ref);
            H_blok_ref = H_blok_ref / vsota_blok_ref;

            % intersekcija/presek

            presek = 0;  
            for k = 1:length(H_ref)
                presek = presek + min(H_ref(k), H_blok_ref(k));
            end
            
            siv_vrednost = uint8(255 * presek);   % 255 namesto 256, da ne pride do preliva 
            
            % vsem pikslom v bloku pripišemo isto sivinsko vrednost
            siv_frame(vrstica_zac:vrstica_kon, stolpec_zac:stolpec_kon) = siv_vrednost;
        end
    end

    writeVideo(video_izhod, siv_frame);
end

close(video_izhod);
implay('ladja_intersection.avi');

==== BEGIN Tina Gutnik | Tina Gutnik_21495_assignsubmission_file | sivinska_slika_komponent.m ====
function sivinska_slika_komponent(datoteka)
% vhod datoteke mora biti v obliki 'slika.jpg'
% funkcija vrne 6 sivinskih slik - vsako izmed HSV in Lab komponent


I_int = imread(datoteka);
I_double = im2double(I_int);

% HSV prostor

I_hsv = rgb2hsv(I_double);   % pretvorimo v HSV prostor

H = I_hsv(:,:,1); 
S = I_hsv(:,:,2); 
V = I_hsv(:,:,3);

figure;                  
imagesc(H);
colormap(gray);
title('HSV: H');

figure;                  
imagesc(S);
colormap(gray);
title('HSV: S');

figure;                  
imagesc(V);
colormap(gray);
title('HSV: V');

% Lab prostor

I_lab = rgb2lab(I_double);

L = I_lab(:,:,1); 
a = I_lab(:,:,2);   
b = I_lab(:,:,3);  

figure;                  
imagesc(L);
colormap(gray);
title('Lab: L');

figure;                  
imagesc(a);
colormap(gray);
title('Lab: a');

figure;                  
imagesc(b);
colormap(gray);
title('Lab: b');

end

close all;
clear;

yosemite_HSV = rgb2hsv(yosemite_RGB);

sliki_HSV{1} = yosemite_HSV;
sliki_HSV{2} = pencils_HSV;

sliki_LAB{1} = yosemite_LAB;
sliki_LAB{2} = pencils_LAB;

%% 1 HSV

oznake_komponent = ["hue", "saturation", "value"];

for i = 1:3

    figure;
    imagesc(yosemite_HSV(:, :, i));
    colormap(gray);
    title(sprintf('yosemite\n%s', oznake_komponent(i)));

    figure;
    imagesc(pencils_HSV(:, :, i));
    colormap(gray);
    title(sprintf('pencils\n%s', oznake_komponent(i)));

end

for i = 1:3

    figure;
    imagesc(yosemite_LAB(:, :, i));
    colormap(gray);
    title(sprintf('yosemite\n%s', oznake_komponent(i)));

    figure;
    imagesc(pencils_LAB(:, :, i));
    colormap(gray);
    title(sprintf('pencils\n%s', oznake_komponent(i)));

end

%% 2 – imena slik za naslove

imena_slik = {'yosemite', 'pencils'};

%% 2 HSV H=konst

for i = 1:2

    x = sliki_HSV{i};
    x(:, :, 1) = mean(x(:, :, 1), 'all');
    x_RGB = hsv2rgb(x);
    figure;
    imshow(x_RGB);

end

    x(:, :, 2) = mean(x(:, :, 2), 'all');
    x_RGB = hsv2rgb(x);
    figure;
    imshow(x_RGB);
    title(sprintf('%s\nHSV, S = konst', imena_slik{i}));

end

% 2 HSV V=konst

for i = 1:2

    x = sliki_HSV{i};
    x(:, :, 3) = mean(x(:, :, 3), 'all');
    x_RGB = hsv2rgb(x);
    figure;
    imshow(x_RGB);
    title(sprintf('%s\nHSV, V = konst', imena_slik{i}));

end

    x = sliki_LAB{i};
